### [1] [On the potential of pseudo-scalar dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09994)
*Andrea Minotti,Yunzhi Wu,Marco Regis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates pseudo-scalar dark energy models coupled to photons, which induce cosmic birefringence (CB). By combining background expansion data with CB measurements, the authors constrain model parameters and find that axion-like potentials require large anomaly coefficients, while quadratic, linear, and Ratra-Peebles potentials can simultaneously explain dark energy and CB with symmetry-breaking scales near the GUT scale.
\n\n
本文研究了与光子耦合的赝标量暗能量模型，该模型会引发宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence, CB）。作者结合宇宙背景膨胀历史观测与CB测量数据，对模型参数进行限制，发现类轴子势仅在反常系数较大时可行，而二次型、线性及Ratra-Peebles势可在对称性破缺能标接近大统一理论（GUT）能标的情况下同时解释暗能量和CB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to explain dynamical dark energy through a cosmological pseudo-scalar field, which naturally couples to photons via a Chern-Simons term. This coupling induces cosmic birefringence—an observable rotation of CMB polarization—providing a direct observational probe of the field’s dynamics. The paper aims to test whether specific pseudo-scalar potentials can simultaneously account for late-time cosmic acceleration and recent CB signals.
\n\n
动机源于通过宇宙学赝标量场解释动力学暗能量的需求，该场通过Chern-Simons项自然地与光子耦合。这种耦合会引发宇宙微波背景（CMB）偏振面的旋转，即宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence），为该场的动力学提供了直接的观测探针。本文旨在检验特定的赝标量势能否同时解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀和近期观测到的CB信号。

Method: The authors analyze several pseudo-scalar dark energy models characterized by different scalar potentials: axion-like ($V(\\phi) \\propto 1 - \\cos(\\phi/f)$), quadratic ($V \\propto \\phi^2$), linear ($V \\propto \\phi$), and Ratra-Peebles ($V \\propto \\phi^{-\\alpha}$). They compute the evolution of the pseudo-scalar field $\\phi(t)$ in a Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker (FLRW) background and relate its time variation to the cosmic birefringence angle $\\beta = \\frac{g}{4} (\\phi_0 - \\phi_{\\rm last})$, where $g$ is the photon coupling constant. Constraints are derived by jointly fitting background expansion data (e.g., SNIa, BAO, $H_0$) and CB measurements (e.g., from Planck or other CMB experiments).
\n\n
作者分析了几种具有不同标量势的赝标量暗能量模型：类轴子势（$V(\\phi) \\propto 1 - \\cos(\\phi/f)$）、二次势（$V \\propto \\phi^2$）、线性势（$V \\propto \\phi$）和Ratra-Peebles势（$V \\propto \\phi^{-\\alpha}$）。他们在Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker（FLRW）宇宙背景下计算赝标量场$\\phi(t)$的演化，并将其时间变化与宇宙双折射角$\\beta = \\frac{g}{4} (\\phi_0 - \\phi_{\\rm last})$关联起来，其中$g$为光子耦合常数。约束条件通过联合拟合背景膨胀数据（如Ia型超新星SNIa、重子声学振荡BAO、哈勃常数$H_0$）和CB测量值（如来自Planck或其他CMB实验的数据）得出。

Result: The analysis shows that the axion-like potential is only viable if the anomaly coefficient $g f$ is large (i.e., $g f \\gtrsim \\mathcal{O}(1)$ in reduced Planck units), which may challenge naturalness. In contrast, power-law potentials—quadratic, linear, and Ratra-Peebles—can successfully fit both the dark energy equation of state and observed CB angles, with the symmetry-breaking scale $f$ close to the Grand Unification Theory (GUT) scale ($f \\sim 10^{16}\\,\\text{GeV}$). This suggests a possible connection between dark energy physics and high-energy particle theory.
\n\n
分析表明，类轴子势仅在反常系数$g f$较大时（即在约化普朗克单位下$g f \\gtrsim \\mathcal{O}(1)$）才可行，这可能违背自然性。相比之下，幂律势（二次、线性和Ratra-Peebles）能够同时很好地拟合暗能量状态方程和观测到的CB角度，且其对称性破缺能标$f$接近大统一理论（GUT）能标（$f \\sim 10^{16}\\,\\text{GeV}$）。这暗示暗能量物理可能与高能粒子理论存在联系。

Conclusion: Pseudo-scalar dark energy models with non-minimal photon couplings offer a testable framework linking cosmic acceleration and cosmic birefringence. While axion-like models face tight constraints unless the coupling is unnaturally large, simpler monomial or inverse-power-law potentials remain viable and point toward a high-energy origin near the GUT scale, making them promising targets for future CMB and cosmological surveys.
\n\n
具有非最小光子耦合的赝标量暗能量模型提供了一个可检验的框架，将宇宙加速膨胀与宇宙双折射联系起来。尽管类轴子模型在耦合不特别大的情况下受到严格限制，但更简单的单项式或反幂律势仍然可行，并指向接近GUT能标的高能起源，使其成为未来CMB和宇宙学巡天的重要目标。

Abstract: A cosmological pseudo-scalar field provides a compelling realization of dynamical dark energy (DE). If its coupling to photons is non-negligible, the cosmic microwave background acquires a rotation of its polarization plane, known as cosmic birefringence (CB). We present an extended analysis of several pseudo-scalar DE models and derive constraints on the parameters of their potentials by combining observations of the background expansion history with measurements of CB. We find that the axion-like potential constitutes a viable model only for large values of the anomaly coefficient. Scenarios in which the pseudo-scalar field rolls down a potential with quadratic, linear, or Ratra-Peebles forms can successfully explain DE and CB, with a symmetry-breaking scale close to the GUT scale.

</details>


### [2] [A universal model for the accretion rates and formation times of dark matter halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09997)
*Ankita Bera,Benedikt Diemer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The paper presents a universal six-parameter fitting function for the median mass accretion rate (MAR) of dark matter halos based on three physical variables—peak height $ν$, effective spectral index $n_{\rm eff}$, and effective linear growth rate $α_{\rm eff}$—validated across cosmologies ($Λ$CDM and Einstein–de Sitter), redshifts ($z = 0$–$14$), and simulation types (dark matter-only and hydrodynamical). A two-parameter fit for formation redshift also improves upon the Lacey & Cole model. The functions are implemented in the public \textsc{Colossus} toolkit.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于三个物理变量——峰值高度 $ν$、有效谱指数 $n_{\rm eff}$ 和有效线性增长率 $α_{\rm eff}$——的六参数普适拟合函数，用于描述暗物质晕的中值质量吸积率（MAR）。该模型在不同宇宙学模型（$Λ$CDM 和爱因斯坦–德西特宇宙）、红移范围（$z = 0$–$14$）以及模拟类型（纯暗物质与流体动力学模拟）中均得到验证。此外，一个两参数的形成红移拟合公式改进了 Lacey & Cole 模型。相关函数已集成至公开的 \textsc{Colossus} 工具包中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing models of halo mass accretion rates (MARs) lack universality and simplicity, especially at high redshifts and low masses. Despite their importance for galaxy formation, no accurate and broadly applicable analytic description of MARs has been established across different cosmologies and simulation methodologies.
\n\n
现有的晕质量吸积率（MAR）模型缺乏普适性和简洁性，尤其在高红移和低质量区域表现不佳。尽管 MAR 对星系形成至关重要，但目前尚无一种能在不同宇宙学模型和模拟方法下广泛适用且精确的解析描述。

Method: The authors measure median MARs and half-mass formation times from both dark matter-only and hydrodynamical simulations across $z = 0$–$14$ in both $Λ$CDM and Einstein–de Sitter cosmologies. They identify three key physical variables—peak height $ν$, effective slope of the linear power spectrum $n_{\rm eff}$, and effective linear growth rate $α_{\rm eff}$—and construct a six-parameter fitting function for MAR. Additionally, they propose a two-parameter fit for formation redshift, improving on the Lacey & Cole formalism by fixing one parameter to its physical value and incorporating $n_{\rm eff}$ dependence.
\n\n
作者从 $Λ$CDM 和爱因斯坦–德西特宇宙学下的纯暗物质及流体动力学模拟中测量了红移 $z = 0$–$14$ 范围内的中值 MAR 和半质量形成时间。他们识别出三个关键物理变量：峰值高度 $ν$、线性功率谱的有效斜率 $n_{\rm eff}$ 和有效线性增长率 $α_{\rm eff}$，并构建了一个六参数 MAR 拟合函数。此外，他们还提出了一个两参数的形成红移拟合公式，通过将一个参数固定为其物理值并引入对 $n_{\rm eff}$ 的依赖，改进了 Lacey & Cole 的形式。

Result: The measured MARs are found to be nearly identical in hydrodynamical and dark matter-only simulations, confirming that baryonic physics has minimal impact on halo accretion histories. The six-parameter function accurately captures MARs over a wide dynamic range in mass, redshift, and cosmology. The two-parameter formation redshift fit outperforms previous models, particularly at high $z$ and low mass. The new fits are consistent with some existing prescriptions but extend their validity and improve accuracy.
\n\n
测量结果表明，流体动力学模拟与纯暗物质模拟中的 MAR 几乎一致，证实重子物理对晕吸积历史影响甚微。六参数函数在大范围的质量、红移和宇宙学参数空间中均能精确描述 MAR。两参数的形成红移拟合在高红移和低质量区域优于以往模型。新拟合公式与部分现有方案一致，但显著拓展了适用范围并提高了精度。

Conclusion: A universal, physically motivated parametrization of halo mass accretion rates is achievable using only three fundamental variables derived from linear theory. This provides a robust baseline for modeling galaxy formation and evolution across cosmic time, with practical implementations available via the \textsc{Colossus} toolkit.
\n\n
使用仅来自线性理论的三个基本变量，即可实现对晕质量吸积率的普适且具有物理动机的参数化。这为跨宇宙时间尺度的星系形成与演化建模提供了稳健基准，相关实用工具已通过 \textsc{Colossus} 工具包提供。

Abstract: The formation histories of halos set the baseline rate at which galaxies accrete gas over cosmic time. While a number of models describe these histories and their derivative, the mass accretion rate (MAR), a simple and universal formula has remained elusive. Here we measure the median MARs and half-mass formation times of halos in dark matter-only and hydrodynamical simulations, in extremely different cosmologies ($Λ$CDM and Einstein-de Sitter), and across a wide range of redshifts ($z = 0$-$14$). We confirm that MARs increase with mass and redshift, and that they are virtually identical in hydrodynamical and dark matter-only simulations. We show that MARs are accurately described by a universal six-parameter function of three physical variables: the peak height $ν$, the slope of the linear power spectrum $n_{\rm eff}$, and the effective linear growth rate $α_{\rm eff}$. A complementary two-parameter fit for the formation redshift improves on the function of Lacey \& Cole by fixing one parameter to its physical value and adding a dependence on $n_{\rm eff}$. Our model is broadly consistent with some prescriptions from the literature but provides a larger range and higher accuracy at high redshifts and low masses. Our fitting functions are implemented in the publicly available \textsc{Colossus} toolkit.

</details>


### [3] [Mixed Dark Matter: Limits from the Milky Way Satellite Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10006)
*Wendy Crumrine,Dominic Pang,Ethan O. Nadler,Andrew Benson,Vera Gluscevic*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates two-component dark matter (DM) models where cold dark matter (CDM) is mixed with either fuzzy DM (FDM) or interacting DM (IDM). Using Milky Way satellite galaxy counts, the authors place leading constraints on FDM particle mass and IDM scattering cross sections for non-CDM fractions as low as 50%. Bounds weaken with decreasing beyond-CDM fraction following power-law trends. Future LSST-like surveys could significantly tighten these limits, but robust interpretation will require cosmological simulations of mixed DM scenarios.
\n\n
本文研究了双组分暗物质（DM）模型，其中冷暗物质（CDM）与模糊暗物质（FDM）或相互作用暗物质（IDM）混合。作者利用银河系卫星星系的数量，对非CDM组分低至50%的情形设定了当前最强的FDM粒子质量与IDM散射截面限制。随着超越CDM组分比例的降低，限制按幂律趋势减弱。未来的LSST类巡天有望显著收紧这些限制，但对其稳健解释将依赖于混合DM场景的自洽宇宙学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Standard Model’s particle diversity suggests that the dark sector may also be complex. Single-component DM models like pure CDM face challenges on small scales (e.g., missing satellites problem), motivating mixed DM scenarios where one component suppresses small-scale structure—either via quantum pressure (FDM) or collisional damping (IDM). The paper aims to systematically constrain such mixed models using astrophysical observables, particularly Milky Way satellite abundances, which are sensitive to suppression in the linear matter power spectrum.
\n\n
标准模型中粒子种类的多样性暗示暗物质领域也可能具有复杂结构。纯冷暗物质（CDM）等单组分模型在小尺度上面临挑战（如缺失卫星星系问题），这促使人们考虑混合暗物质模型：其中一个组分通过量子压强（FDM）或碰撞阻尼（IDM）抑制小尺度结构形成。本文旨在利用对线性物质功率谱抑制敏感的天体物理观测量（尤其是银河系卫星星系丰度）系统性地约束此类混合模型。

Method: The authors compare the linear matter power spectra of mixed DM models (50–100% CDM + complementary FDM or IDM) against a reference model whose small-scale suppression is already observationally excluded. They require that viable models exhibit less suppression than this reference. Using the observed number of Milky Way satellites as a proxy for small-scale power, they derive 95% confidence upper limits on the IDM scattering cross section (for couplings to photons $γ$-DM, neutrinos $ν$-DM, and baryons $p$-DM) and lower limits on the FDM particle mass, as functions of the non-CDM fraction. They also forecast improvements from idealized future surveys mimicking LSST sensitivity.
\n\n
作者将混合暗物质模型（50–100% CDM + 互补的FDM或IDM）的线性物质功率谱与一个已被观测排除的小尺度抑制参考模型进行比较，并要求可行模型的抑制程度弱于该参考模型。他们以观测到的银河系卫星星系数量作为小尺度功率的代理量，推导出95%置信度下IDM散射截面的上限（针对与光子$γ$-DM、中微子$ν$-DM和重子$p$-DM的耦合）以及FDM粒子质量的下限，这些限制是作为非CDM组分比例的函数给出的。他们还预测了模拟LSST灵敏度的理想化未来巡天所能带来的改进。

Result: For mixed FDM and IDM models with 50–100% CDM, the 95% confidence bounds on particle parameters weaken as the beyond-CDM fraction decreases. Specifically, at 50% non-CDM fraction, FDM mass bounds weaken by ~1.5× and IDM cross section bounds by ~2–6× compared to the 100% case. These weakenings follow distinct power-law scalings with fraction. Future LSST-like surveys could improve 100% bounds by factors of ~1.6–14 for IDM and ~3 for FDM. Constraints are derived for three IDM coupling channels: $γ$-DM, $ν$-DM, and $p$-DM.
\n\n
对于含50–100% CDM的混合FDM与IDM模型，随着超越CDM组分比例的降低，粒子参数的95%置信度限制随之减弱。具体而言，在50%非CDM比例下，FDM质量限制比100%情形弱约1.5倍，而IDM截面限制弱约2–6倍。这种减弱遵循与组分比例相关的不同幂律标度。未来的LSST类巡天可将100%情形的限制提升1.6–14倍（IDM）和约3倍（FDM）。本文给出了三种IDM耦合通道（$γ$-DM、$ν$-DM和$p$-DM）的限制。

Conclusion: Mixed dark matter scenarios introduce degeneracies between particle physics parameters and the fractional contribution of non-CDM components, weakening observational constraints as the CDM fraction increases. While current satellite counts provide leading limits down to 50% non-CDM, robustly extending these constraints—and identifying additional observational signatures—will require self-consistent cosmological simulations of mixed DM models. Future surveys hold significant promise for tightening bounds, especially for IDM.
\n\n
混合暗物质模型在粒子物理参数与非CDM组分比例之间引入简并性，导致观测限制随CDM比例增加而减弱。尽管当前卫星星系计数已对低至50%非CDM组分提供了领先限制，但要稳健地外推这些限制并识别额外观测特征，仍需开展混合DM模型的自洽宇宙学模拟。未来巡天在收紧限制方面（尤其是对IDM）具有显著潜力。

Abstract: The Standard Model of particle physics contains a diverse set of particle species, motivating the possibility of a similarly complex dark sector. Here we study two-component dark matter (DM) mixtures, in which one component behaves as standard CDM while the other suppresses the formation of small-scale structure, either through an astrophysically relevant de~Broglie wavelength (fuzzy DM; FDM) or collisional damping from temperature-independent scattering (interacting DM; IDM). Using the observed population of Milky Way satellite galaxies, we derive new leading constraints on the parameter spaces of mixed FDM and of mixed IDM coupled to photons ($γ$-DM), neutrinos ($ν$-DM), or baryons ($p$-DM), for beyond-CDM fractions down to $50\%$. We require that the linear matter power spectra of allowed models remain less suppressed than a constrained reference model. The resulting $95\%$ confidence bounds on FDM mass and IDM cross section weaken systematically with decreasing fraction, following distinct power-law scalings. At $50\%$ fraction, IDM cross section bounds weaken by a factor of $\sim$2--6 and FDM mass bounds by $\sim$1.5, relative to the $100\%$ case. We forecast that idealized future satellite surveys, which adopt approximate LSST sensitivity thresholds, can improve these $100\%$ bounds by a factor of $\sim$1.6--14 for IDM and $\sim$3 for FDM. Self-consistent cosmological simulations of mixed DM scenarios will be essential to more robustly characterize the degeneracy between particle physics parameters and fractional contribution, to extend constraints to lower fractions, and to identify signatures beyond satellite abundance to further inform these models.

</details>


### [4] [The Manticore Project II: Bayesian digital twins of cosmic structure across the SDSS and BOSS volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10020)
*Stuart McAlpine,Jens Jasche,Guilhem Lavaux,Ludvig Doeser,Arthur Loureiro*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Manticore-Deep presents a high-resolution Bayesian reconstruction of the cosmic large-scale structure over $(4~h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc})^{3}$ out to $z \approx 0.7$, using a joint analysis of five galaxy surveys within a hierarchical Bayesian framework (BORG). It infers initial Gaussian conditions evolved via gravity, yielding posterior ensembles consistent with LCDM and validated by CMB lensing ($7.4σ$) and kSZ ($3.5σ$) signals.
\n\n
Manticore-Deep 提出了一种高分辨率贝叶斯重建方法，覆盖 $(4~h^{-1}\mathrm{Gpc})^{3}$ 的共动体积并延伸至 $z \approx 0.7$，通过在分层贝叶斯框架（BORG 算法）内联合分析五个星系巡天数据，推断出符合 LCDM 的高斯初始条件，并通过引力演化生成后验密度与速度场集合。该结果通过 CMB 弱引力透镜（$7.4σ$）和 kSZ 效应（$3.5σ$）得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to overcome limitations in previous large-scale structure reconstructions—such as restricted volume, resolution, or reliance on phenomenological models—by performing a field-level, physically motivated Bayesian inference that jointly incorporates multiple redshift surveys. This enables a more complete, self-consistent, and cosmologically faithful reconstruction of the cosmic web back to primordial initial conditions.
\n\n
本文旨在克服以往大尺度结构重建在体积、分辨率或对唯象模型依赖等方面的局限性，通过在物理驱动的贝叶斯框架下联合多个红移巡天数据，实现对宇宙网从当前观测回溯至原初初始条件的完整、自洽且符合宇宙学原理的场级别重建。

Method: Manticore-Deep employs the BORG (Bayesian Origin Reconstruction from Galaxies) algorithm within a hierarchical Bayesian framework to jointly analyze five galaxy redshift surveys (2M++, 6dFGS, 2dFGRS, SDSS, BOSS). It infers the primordial density field $\\delta_{\\mathrm{ini}}(\\mathbf{x})$, assumed Gaussian and isotropic, which is then evolved forward using Lagrangian perturbation theory (LPT) or particle-mesh N-body methods to match observed galaxy distributions via a likelihood model incorporating survey geometry and selection effects. A novel tiled inference strategy partitions the large volume into manageable subvolumes, enabling feasible computation at ${\\sim}4$~Mpc/h resolution over $(4~h^{-1}\\mathrm{Gpc})^{3}$. Posterior samples are generated via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo.
\n\n
Manticore-Deep 在分层贝叶斯框架中采用 BORG（Bayesian Origin Reconstruction from Galaxies）算法，联合分析五个星系红移巡天（2M++、6dFGS、2dFGRS、SDSS、BOSS）。该方法推断原初密度场 $\\delta_{\\mathrm{ini}}(\\mathbf{x})$（假设为高斯且各向同性），并通过拉格朗日微扰理论（LPT）或粒子网格 N-body 方法将其向前演化，以匹配考虑了巡天几何与选择效应的观测星系分布。一种新颖的分块（tiled）推断策略将大体积划分为可计算的子区域，使得在 $(4~h^{-1}\\mathrm{Gpc})^{3}$ 体积内以 ${\\sim}4$~Mpc/h 分辨率进行计算成为可能。后验样本通过哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo）生成。

Result: The posterior ensemble of reconstructed density and velocity fields is statistically consistent with LCDM: initial conditions are Gaussian and isotropic, and late-time statistics (matter power spectrum, bispectrum, halo mass function) match LCDM predictions on resolved scales. Independent validation yields a $7.4σ$ cross-correlation with Planck CMB lensing and a $3.5σ$ kinematic Sunyaev-Zel’dovich (kSZ) detection from velocity-weighted stacking of 64,750 clusters. The inferred velocities are confirmed to align with true cluster motions via a recession-split test. The BOSS Great Wall appears as a ${\\sim}3σ$ overdensity consistent across all realizations.
\n\n
重建的密度场与速度场后验集合在统计上与 LCDM 一致：初始条件呈高斯且各向同性，晚期统计量（物质功率谱、双谱、晕质量函数）在已解析尺度上符合 LCDM 预期。独立验证显示：与 Planck CMB 弱引力透镜图的互相关达到 $7.4σ$ 显著性；通过对 64,750 个星系团进行速度加权堆叠，在 Planck 217 GHz 图上探测到 $3.5σ$ 的动力学 Sunyaev-Zel’dovich（kSZ）信号；通过退行分割（recession split）检验确认推断速度与真实团块运动方向一致。BOSS 巨墙在所有后验实现中均表现为 ${\\sim}3σ$ 的超密度结构，符合 LCDM。

Conclusion: Manticore-Deep demonstrates that large-volume, high-resolution Bayesian field-level inference of cosmic structure is computationally feasible and physically robust. By jointly analyzing multiple surveys and validating against independent cosmological probes (CMB lensing and kSZ), it provides a powerful framework for testing fundamental cosmological assumptions, reconstructing initial conditions, and enabling precision studies of structure formation within LCDM.
\n\n

作者以IllustrisTNG模型为基础生成1,192个宇宙学流体动力学模拟，变化35个参数，包括宇宙学参数（如$\\Omega_m$、$\\sigma_8$等）、天体物理参数（恒星/AGN反馈强度）、数值参数（分辨率）以及四个新引入的控制紫外/ X射线电离背景的参数。每个模拟的盒子尺寸为$(50 \\, \\text{Mpc}/h)^3$。他们构建了四类可观测量：(1) 物质功率谱$P(k)$，(2) 密度/温度的二维投影图，(3) 编码星系位置与性质的图结构，(4) 大质量晕的热力学轮廓。相应地，他们分别应用多层感知机（MLP）、卷积神经网络（CNN）、图神经网络（GNN）和高斯过程（GP）进行参数推断。结果与在旧版$(25 \\, \\text{Mpc}/h)^3$ CAMELS模拟上进行的相同分析对比，以分离体积效应。

Result: Larger simulation volumes yield tighter marginal posterior constraints on both cosmological and astrophysical parameters across all data types, confirming reduced sample variance. However, the improvement in constraint tightness scales sublinearly with volume—less than the expected $\\propto V^{1/2}$ scaling from Gaussian statistics—suggesting information loss from nonlinear mode coupling (e.g., in EFTofLSS) or complex parameter degeneracies. Among data modalities, CNNs on projected maps and GNNs on galaxy graphs show the most significant gains. Additionally, the four new ionizing background parameters significantly affect the temperature distribution of the intergalactic medium (IGM), particularly at $z \\sim 2{-}6$, demonstrating their importance in modeling IGM thermodynamics.

更大的模拟体积在所有数据类型上均带来了对宇宙学和天体物理参数更紧的边缘后验约束，证实了样本方差的降低。然而，约束收紧程度随体积的增长呈次线性关系——弱于高斯统计预期的$\\propto V^{1/2}$标度——表明信息因非线性模式耦合（例如在EFTofLSS中）或复杂的参数简并而损失。在各类数据模态中，基于投影图的CNN和基于星系图的GNN表现出最显著的增益。此外，四个新引入的电离背景参数显著影响星系际介质（IGM）的温度分布，尤其在红移$z \\sim 2{-}6$区间，凸显了它们在IGM热力学建模中的重要性。

Conclusion: Increasing simulation volume substantially improves parameter inference fidelity in cosmological simulations with baryonic physics, but diminishing returns arise due to nonlinear gravitational evolution and parameter degeneracies. The work underscores the need for joint modeling of large-scale structure and small-scale astrophysics, and highlights the complementary roles of different neural architectures in extracting information from diverse data representations. The public release of this large, varied simulation suite enables future studies of emulator robustness, summary statistics, and multi-messenger cosmology.

增大模拟体积显著提升了包含重子物理的宇宙学模拟中参数推断的可靠性，但由于非线性引力演化和参数简并，收益递减。本工作强调了大尺度结构与小尺度天体物理联合建模的必要性，并突显了不同神经网络架构在从多样化数据表示中提取信息时的互补作用。该大型、多样化的模拟数据集的公开发布，将推动未来关于模拟器鲁棒性、汇总统计量及多信使宇宙学的研究。

Abstract: We present a new set of 1,192 cosmological simulations as part of the CAMELS project, in which a space of 35 cosmological, astrophysical, and numerical parameters is explored around the fiducial IllustrisTNG model. The volume of each of these simulations is (50Mpc/h)^3, eight times larger than that of previous CAMELS simulations. This provides lower sample variance as well as access to more massive halos and more diverse environments. We focus this work on exploring the advantages these differences provide for parameter inference powered by neural networks. We generate training sets based on the matter power spectra, projected maps of the volumes, graphs representing galaxy spatial distributions, and thermodynamical properties of massive halos. We employ multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, graph neural networks, and Gaussian processes, respectively, to extract information on the simulation parameters from these inputs while comparing systematically to analogous results from our previous generation of (25Mpc/h)^3 simulations. We generally find that the new, larger volumes produce tighter marginal constraints on the parameters, to degrees that vary between the different inputs. The improvements, however, scale more weakly than with the square root of the increase in the amount of data (i.e., physical volume). We interpret this as originating either from information loss due to mode coupling or from complex degeneracies in parameter space. We also discuss the effects on statistics of the intergalactic medium temperature from four new parameters that are varied in these simulations, which control the amplitude and timing of the ionizing background radiation. We publicly release the simulation outputs and ancillary data at https://camels.readthedocs.io.

Abstract: Merging galaxy clusters represent the ideal laboratory to test our understanding of the large scale structure formation history and the processes involved. While many merging clusters have been identified, only a limited number have been studied in detail through multi-wavelength analysis and dynamical reconstruction, this type of analysis being crucial to account for projection degeneracies.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current tests of gravity using large-scale structure often suffer from parameter degeneracies among different modified gravity (MG) parameters such as $μ$, $η$, and $Σ$. 当前利用大尺度结构对引力的检验常受限于不同Modified Gravity（MG）参数（如$μ$、$η$和$Σ$）之间的参数简并性，这削弱了单一探针（如$fσ_8$（增长速率）或$Σ_8$（物质涨落幅度））的约束能力。引力势衰减率（$\mathit{DR}$）对MG参数的响应方向与其他探针的简并方向不同，提供了互补信息。作者旨在量化：在现象学和基于EFT的框架下，将近期的层析$\mathit{DR}$测量与现有探针联合分析时，约束能力能提升多少。

For $(μ_0,η_0)$, they find $μ_0=0.06^{+0.17}_{-0.23}$ and $η_0=-0.03^{+0.36}_{-0.46}$, with $\sim1.5\times$ improvement from adding $\mathit{DR}$. In the EFT $α$-basis, they obtain $c_{\rm M}=0.64^{+0.32}_{-0.72}$ and $c_{\rm B}=0.31^{+0.19}_{-0.29}$, representing uncertainties $\sim2\times$ smaller than those in \citep{2025JCAP...09..053I}, which used DESI full-shape+BAO, DES-SN5YR, and CMB data but not $\mathit{DR}$.

Abstract: We test gravity by exploiting the synergy between the gravitational potential decay rate ($\mathit{DR}$) and complementary structure-growth probes: these observables respond to MG parameters with different degeneracy directions, so their combination yields stronger constraints than any single probe. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stage IV surveys like LSST will access deeply nonlinear scales where systematic uncertainties from galaxy bias and baryonic feedback dominate. Standard analyses often impose conservative scale cuts or use overly simplistic bias models, risking loss of information or biased cosmological inference. The authors aim to develop a robust framework that simultaneously accounts for nonlinear bias (via HEFT) and baryonic effects (via baryonification), while quantifying degeneracies and assessing the feasibility of detecting subtle signals like the total neutrino mass $M_ν$.
\n\n
第四阶段巡天（如LSST）将深入非线性尺度，此时星系偏差和重子反馈引入的系统误差占主导地位。传统分析常采用保守的尺度截断或过于简化的偏差模型，可能导致信息损失或宇宙学参数推断偏差。作者旨在构建一个稳健框架，同时处理非线性偏差（通过HEFT）和重子效应（通过baryonification），并量化参数简并性，评估探测总中微子质量$M_ν$等微弱信号的可行性。

Method: The authors perform a $3\times2$pt (cosmic shear, galaxy--galaxy lensing, and galaxy clustering) likelihood analysis for LSST Y1 and Y10 using the BACCO emulator. Galaxy bias is modeled using hybrid-effective field theory (HEFT), which combines perturbative bias expansion with empirical calibration. Baryonic feedback is incorporated via the baryonification model, which modifies the matter power spectrum based on hydrodynamical simulation-inspired prescriptions. They compare a full HEFT model with a “minimal bias” variant where higher-order bias parameters are fixed. All computations are carried out using a newly developed open-source pipeline named MGL (Modular Galaxy Lensing).
\n\n
作者基于BACCO模拟器对LSST Y1和Y10数据执行$3\times2$pt（宇宙剪切、星系-星系透镜和星系成团性）似然分析。星系偏差采用hybrid-effective field theory（HEFT）建模，该方法结合了微扰偏差展开与经验校准；重子反馈通过baryonification模型引入，该模型依据流体动力学模拟启发的处方修正物质功率谱。他们将完整HEFT模型与一种“最小偏差”变体（固定高阶偏差参数）进行对比。所有计算均通过新开发的开源管道MGL（Modular Galaxy Lensing）完成。

Result: (1) Linear bias yields unbiased $Ω_{\rm m}$ and $σ_8$ only for $k_{\rm max} \leq 0.1\,h/$Mpc; beyond this, perturbative bias is required.  
(2) The minimal bias model remains unbiased in $Λ$CDM up to $k_{\rm max}=0.7\,h/$Mpc, but higher-order bias terms can mimic baryonic suppression in power spectra, whereas baryons cannot replicate the full behavior of higher-order bias.  
(3) A detection of total neutrino mass $M_ν$ is achievable for both Y1 and Y10 at $k \geq 0.3\,h/$Mpc if photometric redshift uncertainties are well controlled—but the inferred $M_ν$ value is not robust across different bias modeling assumptions, showing significant bias when using the minimal model.
\n\n
（1）线性偏差模型仅在$k_{\rm max} \leq 0.1\,h/$Mpc时能给出无偏的$Ω_{\rm m}$和$σ_8$约束；超出此范围需采用微扰偏差方法。  
（2）在$Λ$CDM下，最小偏差模型即使在$k_{\rm max}=0.7\,h/$Mpc仍保持无偏，但高阶偏差项可在功率谱中模仿重子抑制效应，而重子反馈无法复现高阶偏差的全部行为。  
（3）若测光红移不确定性得到精确控制，则Y1和Y10数据在$k \geq 0.3\,h/$Mpc时均可探测总中微子质量$M_ν$，但其推断值对偏差建模假设不稳健——使用最小偏差模型会导致显著偏差。

Conclusion: Robust cosmological inference from Stage IV surveys requires joint modeling of nonlinear galaxy bias and baryonic feedback. While simplified bias models may appear sufficient on small scales, they risk absorbing physical effects like baryonic suppression or introducing biases in parameters such as $M_ν$. The work underscores the necessity of flexible, physically motivated bias frameworks like HEFT and highlights the importance of open, modular analysis pipelines like MGL for reproducible and transparent cosmology.
\n\n
从第四阶段巡天中获得稳健的宇宙学推断，必须联合建模非线性星系偏差与重子反馈。尽管简化偏差模型在小尺度上看似足够，但它们可能吸收重子抑制等物理效应，或导致$M_ν$等参数的推断偏差。本工作强调了采用如HEFT这类灵活且物理动机明确的偏差框架的必要性，并凸显了MGL等开源模块化分析管道对可重复、透明宇宙学研究的重要性。

Abstract: Stage IV surveys such as LSST will probe deeply into the nonlinear regime, where systematic effects from galaxy bias and baryonic feedback become dominant and poorly constrained nuisance parameters can lead to degeneracies. In this work we present a $3\times2$pt analysis for LSST Y1 and Y10 data using the BACCO emulator for modelling both the hybrid-effective field theory (HEFT) for nonlinear galaxy bias and the baryonic feedback using the baryonification mechanism. We aim to find a balance between model complexity and scale cuts, with particular attention to parameter degeneracies and baryonic feedback effects on the galaxy--matter and galaxy--galaxy power spectra.
  First, we find that a linear bias model delivers percent-level, unbiased constraints on $Ω_{\rm m}$ and $σ_8$ only up to $k_{\rm max}=0.1\,h/$Mpc, but pushing to smaller scales requires a perturbative approach.
  Second, we compare HEFT with a minimal bias variant with fixed higher-order terms, and find that the latter is unbiased in $Λ$CDM even at $k_{\rm max}=0.7\,h/$Mpc. We show that higher-order bias can mimic baryonic suppression, but baryons cannot reproduce the full range of higher-order bias behaviour.
  Third, we find that a detection of the total neutrino mass $M_ν$ is possible for both Y1 and Y10 for $k\geq0.3\,h/$Mpc, at least when photo-$z$ uncertainties and related nuisance parameters are precisely known. However, the specific measured value is not robust across equally plausible mock scenarios: the inferred $M_ν$ can be significantly biased by adopting the minimal bias model.
  The entire analysis is conducted with a new independent, open source pipeline (MGL) that we present for the first time in this work.

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that primordial magnetic fields evolving through decaying MHD turbulence during the radiation-dominated era follow universal isochrones when plotted against a properly defined proper time.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Different magnetogenesis mechanisms generate primordial magnetic fields with varying initial correlation lengths and amplitudes, making it difficult to compare their late-time evolution or observational signatures. 不同的磁起源（magnetogenesis）机制产生的原初磁场具有不同的初始关联长度和振幅，这使得比较它们的晚期演化或观测信号变得困难。作者旨在通过引入一个固有时（proper time），使早期和晚期的等时线（isochrone）对齐，从而为宇宙学磁场建立一种普适的演化描述，实现与模型无关的预测。

Method: The authors perform two-dimensional numerical simulations of decaying magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence in the radiation-dominated era, varying the initial peak position of the magnetic energy spectrum. They assume conservation of anastrophy (the magnetic analog of enstrophy in 2D turbulence) to govern the inverse cascade. The evolution of the largest processed eddy scale is tracked as a function of time, and they fit the data to a linear relation in terms of the Alfvén time $t_A = L / v_A$, where $L$ is the integral scale and $v_A$ the Alfvén speed. The fit yields both a multiplicative factor (relating decay time to Alfvén time) and an additive offset interpreted as the required proper time correction to the nominal simulation time.

作者在辐射主导时期进行了二维衰减磁流体动力学（MHD）湍流的数值模拟，改变磁能谱的初始峰值位置。他们假设anastrophy（二维湍流中enstrophy的磁类比量）守恒来支配逆级联（inverse cascade）。他们追踪最大已处理涡旋尺度随时间的演化，并将数据拟合为Alfvén时间 $t_A = L / v_A$ 的线性关系，其中 $L$ 是积分尺度，$v_A$ 是Alfvén速度。该拟合同时给出一个乘性因子（关联衰减时间与Alfvén时间）和一个加性偏移量，后者被解释为需加到名义模拟时间上的固有时修正。

Result: The simulations reveal that magnetic field configurations from different initial conditions collapse onto universal isochrones when plotted against a properly shifted time variable. The decay time $t_{\\text{decay}}$ is found to be proportional to the Alfvén time, $t_{\\text{decay}} \\approx C \\, t_A$, with $C \\approx 5$–6 from the fit. The time offset in the linear fit provides a robust estimate of the proper time that must be added to the nominal time since the start of the simulation. Furthermore, including a realistic initial velocity field reduces deviations from straight isochrones, enhancing universality even at early times.

模拟结果显示，当以适当平移的时间变量作图时，不同初始条件下的磁场构型会坍缩到普适的等时线上。衰减时间 $t_{\\text{decay}}$ 被发现与Alfvén时间成正比，$t_{\\text{decay}} \\approx C \\, t_A$，拟合得出 $C \\approx 5$–6。线性拟合中的时间偏移量为必须加到模拟起始名义时间上的固有时提供了稳健估计。此外，包含一个符合实际的初始速度场可减少对直线等时线的偏离，即使在早期也增强了普适性。

Conclusion: The evolution of primordial magnetic fields in the early Universe exhibits universal behavior governed by the conservation of anastrophy in 2D MHD turbulence. By defining a proper time that accounts for the initial conditions, all evolutionary tracks align on universal isochrones, enabling a unified framework to relate magnetogenesis models to present-day observables. This approach simplifies the mapping between early-Universe physics and late-time magnetic field properties.
\n\n
早期宇宙中原初磁场的演化在二维MHD湍流中由anastrophy守恒所支配，表现出普适行为。通过定义一个考虑初始条件的固有时，所有演化轨迹都对齐于普适的等时线上，从而为连接磁起源模型与当今可观测量提供了一个统一框架。该方法简化了从早期宇宙物理到晚期磁场性质的映射。

Abstract: During the radiation-dominated era of the Universe, a primordial magnetic field undergoes a turbulent decay while its length scale increases due to an inverse cascade. At later times, the size of the largest processed eddy scales with the Alfvén speed and it describes an isochrone that moves toward larger scales with increasing time. Different magnetogenesis mechanisms produce different initial length scales and field strengths, independently of the nominal generation time. However, we show that for any initial field, a proper time can be determined such that the isochrones at early times are parallel to those at late times. We use two-dimensional numerical simulations of decaying MHD turbulence and vary the initial position of the peak of the magnetic energy spectrum. In this case, the evolution is governed by the conservation of anastrophy. A fit to the Alfvén time yields an accurate estimate of the factor by which the decay time is longer than the Alfvén time, while the offset in the fit provides an additional estimate of the proper time that needs to be added to the nominal time since the beginning of each simulation. We also find that the presence of an initial velocity field of realistic strength helps producing a more straight track. The magnetic field parameters lie on universal isochrones even for early times.

It finds that continuity of extrinsic curvature leads to a Friedmann-type equation interpreted not as new dynamics but as a geometric consistency condition (GCC), ensuring compatibility between local static physics and the evolving cosmological time without requiring additional screening mechanisms.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modifications to the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, such as the generalized cosmological time (GCT) framework with a nontrivial lapse function $N(t)\propto a^{b/4}$, must not disrupt the well-tested stability of local gravitational and laboratory physics. In scalar–tensor theories, this is typically ensured by screening mechanisms. However, GCT is a purely geometric modification without extra scalar degrees of freedom, raising the question of whether local static regions (like stars or laboratories) can be consistently embedded in such a cosmological background. This motivates a rigorous matching analysis using junction conditions.

Method: The authors embed a static Schwarzschild interior spacetime into an expanding GCT–FLRW exterior spacetime. They apply the Israel junction conditions across the boundary hypersurface, demanding continuity of the induced metric and the extrinsic curvature. The absence of a thin shell (i.e., no surface stress-energy) requires the extrinsic curvature to be continuous. By computing these geometric quantities explicitly for both sides and imposing continuity, they derive a constraint relation between the scale factor $a(t)$ and the Hubble parameter $H = \\dot{a}/a$.
\n\n
作者将静态Schwarzschild内部时空嵌入到膨胀的GCT–FLRW外部时空中。他们在边界超曲面上应用Israel junction条件，要求诱导度规和外曲率连续。在无薄壳（即无表面应力-能量）的情况下，外曲率必须连续。通过显式计算两侧的几何量并施加连续性条件，他们导出了尺度因子$a(t)$与Hubble参数$H = \\dot{a}/a$之间的约束关系。

Result: The continuity of the extrinsic curvature yields a Friedmann-type equation: $H^2 + \\frac{k}{a^2} = \\frac{8\\pi G}{3}\\rho - \\frac{b}{4}H^2$, which matches the background equations of motion of the GCT framework. Crucially, this equation is not an independent dynamical law but arises as a geometric consistency condition (GCC) required for smooth matching between the static interior and the time-dependent cosmological exterior. Conclusion: The analysis demonstrates that a locally static region can be consistently embedded in a GCT cosmological background through a geometric matching condition—the geometric consistency condition (GCC)—which ensures compatibility between the evolving cosmological time normalization and fixed local proper time. This provides a mechanism for preserving standard local physics in modified cosmologies with non-standard lapse functions, even in the absence of traditional screening mechanisms, by enforcing a specific relation between cosmic expansion and the lapse parameter $b$.

Abstract: The generalized cosmological time (GCT) framework introduces a modified lapse function, N(t)\propto a^{b/4}, as a geometric extension of the standard FLRW description. Like other departures from $Λ$CDM, such constructions must remain compatible with the observed stability of local gravitational and laboratory physics. In scalar--tensor theories, this compatibility is usually achieved through dynamical screening mechanisms that suppress additional degrees of freedom in dense environments. In this work, we examine whether a locally static spacetime region can be consistently embedded within a cosmological background described by a time-dependent lapse. By embedding a static Schwarzschild interior into an expanding GCT--FLRW exterior, the Israel junction conditions are used to determine the class of background expansions that admit such a matching in the absence of a thin shell. The continuity of the extrinsic curvature yields a Friedmann-type relation that coincides with the GCT background equations of motion. This relation should be interpreted not as a new dynamical equation, but as a geometric consistency condition (GCC) associated with the matching of the two spacetime regions. In this sense, the junction does not introduce new dynamics, but provides a GCC under which a region with fixed local clocks can be embedded in a cosmological spacetime with an evolving lapse. Therefore, the analysis clarifies how locally static gravitational systems can remain compatible with a cosmological time normalization that differs from that of local proper time while preserving the standard description of local physics.

Abstract: We investigate static, spherically symmetric (SS) spacetimes in covariant teleparallel $F(T)$ gravity sourced by nonlinear Chaplygin and polytropic fluids. Using the covariant coframe/spin-connection (CSC) formalism, we derive the corresponding field equations and conservation laws governing admissible matter distributions and nonlinear torsion sectors. A general reconstruction procedure is developed, allowing the systematic determination of teleparallel $F(T)$ models for arbitrary coframe ansätze and fluid equations of state. Focusing on power-law configurations, we obtain several classes of reconstructed solution branches, including constant-radius, compact-object-like, black-hole-like (BH-like), and wormhole-like (WH-like) branches. The Chaplygin sector naturally generates effective dark-energy and exotic-matter regimes capable of supporting traversable wormhole geometries, while the polytropic sector provides physically relevant models for stellar interiors and compact objects. We discuss the associated horizon and throat structures, torsion singularities, energy conditions, and stability properties of the reconstructed branches. The resulting geometries are organized within the Coley--Landry invariant classification framework, highlighting the role of nonlinear torsion corrections in shaping the solution space. Overall, this work provides a unified covariant framework for the construction and interpretation of compact objects, effective cosmological sectors, and regular-core strong-field geometries beyond General Relativity.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: What is the spectrum of gravitational radiation produced by the head-on collision of two equal-mass black holes?   <summary>Details</summary>

作者将近视界径向坐标函数解析延拓至复平面，并通过视界函数倒数在其对应局部表观/俘获视界的单极点处的留数来定义局域Hawking温度。该基于留数的方法首先应用于Vaidya度规（球对称、质量$M(v)$随时间变化），然后推广至Kinnersley度规（非球对称、任意加速）。

对于Vaidya黑洞，该方法复现了已知的局域温度$T = \\frac{1}{8\\pi M(v)}$。对于视界位置同时依赖时间和角坐标的Kinnersley黑洞，该方法给出了一个点依赖的局域Hawking温度，与广义tortoise坐标方法的结果一致，验证了其在球对称之外情形的有效性。

Conclusion: The RVB--residue method provides a robust and natural framework for defining local Hawking temperatures in dynamical and non-spherical black hole spacetimes, as long as temperature is interpreted quasi-locally near the horizon rather than as a global thermodynamic quantity. Abstract: This paper develops a local residue-based extension of the Robson--Villari--Biancalana method for calculating Hawking temperatures of dynamical black holes. This residue prescription is first applied to the Vaidya black hole, where it reproduces the standard local trapping-horizon temperature (T=1/(8πM(v))). The method is then extended to the arbitrarily accelerating Kinnersley black hole, whose horizon depends on time and angular coordinates. In this case, the RVB--residue method yields a point-dependent local Hawking temperature consistent with the generalized tortoise-coordinate approach. Abstract: We investigate the spectral dynamics of a rotating black hole embedded in a Dehnen $(1,4,γ)$ dark matter halo, where quasibound states and superradiant scattering jointly characterize the physical response of the system. Abstract: In this work, we investigate a cosmic inflation model based on a cosine-type potential with a negative cosmological constant. This model originates from a classical solution of the Wheeler-DeWitt equation. The equation of motion for the inflaton field can be solved analytically without relying on approximation schemes, such as the slow-roll conditions. The predictions of the spectral index, the tensor-to-scalar ratio, and the running spectral index are calculated and compared with experimental constraints from Planck Collaboration, Atacama Cosmology Telescope Collaboration (ACT), and Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI).

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore whether realistic anisotropic compact objects (e.g., neutron stars) can be consistently described within $f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity—a class of symmetric teleparallel gravity where gravity arises purely from non-metricity $\mathbb{Q}$ and couples directly to the matter Lagrangian $\mathcal{L}_{m}$. This addresses the viability of such modified gravity theories in modeling astrophysical compact objects beyond General Relativity.
\n\n
该研究旨在探索各向异性致密天体（如中子星）能否在$f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$引力理论中得到自洽描述——这是一类对称远平行引力理论，其中引力完全源于非度规性$\mathbb{Q}$，并与物质拉格朗日量$\mathcal{L}_{m}$直接耦合。此举旨在检验此类修改引力理论在超越广义相对论框架下对天体物理致密天体建模的可行性。

Method: The authors adopt the static spherically symmetric Krori-Barua metric as a non-singular interior solution. They specify a particular functional form of $f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$ to derive explicit modified field equations. The Darmois junction conditions are applied at the boundary between the interior geometry and the exterior Schwarzschild vacuum to fix integration constants. Physical viability is assessed through energy conditions, equations of state, anisotropy measure ($p_t - p_r$), compactness, surface redshift, mass-radius relation, and stability criteria via adiabatic index $\Gamma > 4/3$ and subluminal sound speeds ($0 < v_{r,t}^2 < 1$).
\n\n
作者采用静态球对称的Krori-Barua度规作为无奇点内解，并选定$f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$的具体函数形式以导出显式修正场方程。通过Darmois匹配条件将内部几何与外部Schwarzschild真空解在边界处连接，从而确定积分常数。通过能量条件、状态方程、各向异性度量（$p_t - p_r$）、致密度、表面红移、质量-半径关系以及稳定性判据（绝热指数$\Gamma > 4/3$和亚光速声速$0 < v_{r,t}^2 < 1$）来评估物理解的合理性。

Result: All physical requirements for a realistic stellar model are satisfied: energy conditions hold, pressure and density are positive and monotonically decreasing, anisotropy is well-behaved, surface redshift is within observational bounds, and both radial and tangential sound speeds are subluminal. The adiabatic index exceeds $4/3$, confirming dynamical stability. Thus, stable anisotropic compact stars are viable in $f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity.
\n\n
所有现实恒星模型所需的物理条件均被满足：能量条件成立，压强与密度为正且单调递减，各向异性行为良好，表面红移处于观测允许范围内，径向与横向声速均为亚光速。绝热指数超过$4/3$，证实了动力学稳定性。因此，在$f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$引力中，稳定的各向异性致密星体是可行的。

Conclusion: The study confirms that $f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity—where non-metricity couples non-minimally to matter—admits physically admissible, stable, anisotropic compact stellar solutions. This supports the theory’s potential as a viable alternative to General Relativity in strong-field regimes.
\n\n
本研究证实，在非度规性与物质非最小耦合的$f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$引力中，存在物理上可接受、稳定且各向异性的致密恒星解。这支持该理论作为广义相对论在强场区域中的一个可行替代理论。

Abstract: This manuscript discusses feasible features of anisotropic celestial sphere within the framework of $f(\mathbb{Q},\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity, where $\mathbb{Q}$ represents non-metricity scalar and $\mathcal{L}_{m}$ is the matter Lagrangian. The geometric configuration of static spherical symmetric structure is examined using a specific non-singular solution (Krori-Barua solution). A particular model of this theory is considered to derive explicit field equations. The Darmois matching conditions are used to evaluate unknown constants in the metric coefficients. To verify plausible existence of compact objects in this gravitational framework, we analyze their fundamental physical properties including fluid parameters, gradients, surface redshift, mass-radius relation, anisotropy measure, compactness factor, energy conditions and equations of state. The stability of the considered stellar objects is verified by adiabatic index and sound speed. Our results demonstrate that all required physical conditions are satisfied, confirming the existence of physically stable anisotropic celestial objects within this modified gravity.

</details>


### [21] [Hawking-Page phase transition for pure Lovelock black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10647)
*Nitesh K. Dubey,Sanved Kolekar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies thermodynamic properties of static, spherically symmetric Anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes in pure Lovelock gravity, focusing on the relationship between the minimum temperature and the Hawking-Page (HP) phase transition temperature, and on the universality of the normalized Ruppeiner scalar curvature at the HP transition. In Einstein gravity, exact relations exist between these temperatures and universal behavior of curvature, but in higher-order pure Lovelock theories, these relations acquire corrections dependent on spacetime dimension and Lovelock order, though they reduce to Einstein results in appropriate limits.
\n\n
本文研究了纯Lovelock引力中静态球对称反德西特（AdS）黑洞的热力学性质，重点关注最低温度与Hawking-Page（HP）相变温度之间的关系，以及HP相变处归一化Ruppeiner标量曲率的普适性。在爱因斯坦引力中，这些温度之间存在精确关系，且曲率表现出普适行为；但在高阶纯Lovelock理论中，这些关系会获得依赖于时空维度和Lovelock阶数的修正，尽管在适当极限下仍可还原为爱因斯坦情形的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how thermodynamic features of AdS black holes—particularly the connection between extremal temperatures and phase transitions, and geometric thermodynamic quantities like the Ruppeiner scalar curvature—generalize beyond Einstein gravity to higher-curvature pure Lovelock theories. Such generalizations test the robustness of thermodynamic universality and may reveal deeper structural properties of gravitational theories with higher-order curvature corrections.
\n\n
动机在于理解AdS黑洞的热力学特征——特别是极值温度与相变之间的联系，以及Ruppeiner标量曲率等几何热力学量——如何从爱因斯坦引力推广到高阶曲率的纯Lovelock理论。这类推广可检验热力学普适性的稳健性，并可能揭示具有高阶曲率修正的引力理论的更深层结构特性。

Method: The authors analyze static, spherically symmetric AdS black hole solutions in pure Lovelock gravity, both neutral and charged. They compute the minimum temperature $T_{\\text{min}}$ from the black hole equation of state and compare it with the Hawking-Page transition temperature $T_{\\text{HP}}$. They also calculate the Ruppeiner metric from the entropy as a function of thermodynamic variables and evaluate its scalar curvature $R$, normalized appropriately, at the HP transition point. The analysis is performed in arbitrary spacetime dimension $d+1$, and comparisons are made between Einstein gravity ($k=1$) and higher-order pure Lovelock theories ($k>1$).
\n\n
作者分析了纯Lovelock引力中静态球对称AdS黑洞解（包括中性和带电情形），从黑洞状态方程计算最低温度 $T_{\\text{min}}$，并与Hawking-Page相变温度 $T_{\\text{HP}}$ 进行比较。他们还基于熵作为热力学变量的函数构建Ruppeiner度规，并在HP相变点计算其标量曲率 $R$（经适当归一化）。分析在任意时空维度 $d+1$ 下进行，并比较爱因斯坦引力（$k=1$）与高阶纯Lovelock理论（$k>1$）的情形。

Result: For neutral black holes in Einstein gravity, $T_{\\text{min}}^{(d+1)} = T_{\\text{HP}}^{(d)}$, while in pure Lovelock gravity of order $k$, this relation becomes $T_{\\text{min}}^{(d+1)} = \\alpha_{d,k} T_{\\text{HP}}^{(d)}$, where $\\alpha_{d,k}$ is a dimension- and order-dependent factor that reduces to 1 when $k=1$. For charged black holes in the grand canonical ensemble in Einstein gravity, $T_{\\text{min}}$ and $T_{\\text{HP}}$ differ by a simple dimension-dependent factor, but no such universal relation exists in higher-order Lovelock theories. The normalized Ruppeiner scalar curvature at the HP transition is a universal constant depending only on $d$ for neutral black holes in all pure Lovelock theories. For charged Einstein black holes, it remains constant under the grand canonical ensemble, but in general pure Lovelock theories it depends on pressure $P$ and electrostatic potential $\\Phi$, approaching a constant only asymptotically as $P \\to \\infty$ or $(\\Phi, P) \\to (\\infty, \\infty)$.
\n\n
对于爱因斯坦引力中的中性黑洞，有 $T_{\\text{min}}^{(d+1)} = T_{\\text{HP}}^{(d)}$；而在 $k$ 阶纯Lovelock引力中，该关系变为 $T_{\\text{min}}^{(d+1)} = \\alpha_{d,k} T_{\\text{HP}}^{(d)}$，其中 $\\alpha_{d,k}$ 是依赖于维度和阶数的因子，在 $k=1$ 时还原为1。对于正则系综中的带电爱因斯坦黑洞，$T_{\\text{min}}$ 与 $T_{\\text{HP}}$ 相差一个仅依赖维度的简单因子，但在高阶Lovelock理论中不存在此类普适关系。对于所有纯Lovelock理论中的中性黑洞，HP相变处的归一化Ruppeiner标量曲率为仅依赖 $d$ 的普适常数。对于带电爱因斯坦黑洞，该曲率在正则系综下仍为常数；但在一般纯Lovelock理论中，它依赖于压强 $P$ 和静电势 $\\Phi$，仅在 $P \\to \\infty$ 或 $(\\Phi, P) \\to (\\infty, \\infty)$ 的渐近极限下趋于常数。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that while certain thermodynamic relations and geometric universality observed in Einstein gravity—such as the equality of minimum and HP transition temperatures across dimensions and constant Ruppeiner curvature at phase transition—extend partially to pure Lovelock gravity, they generally acquire nontrivial corrections. These corrections encode information about the higher-curvature nature of the theory and vanish in the Einstein limit. Thus, thermodynamic geometry provides a sensitive probe of gravitational Lagrangian structure beyond general relativity.
\n\n
本文表明，爱因斯坦引力中观察到的某些热力学关系与几何普适性（如跨维度的最低温度与HP相变温度相等、相变点处Ruppeiner曲率为常数）虽可在纯Lovelock引力中部分推广，但通常会获得非平凡修正。这些修正编码了理论高阶曲率特性的信息，并在爱因斯坦极限下消失。因此，热力学几何为探测广义相对论之外的引力拉格朗日结构提供了灵敏工具。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties of static, spherically symmetric Anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes, focusing on the interplay between characteristic temperatures, as well as on the universality of Ruppeiner scalar curvature at the Hawking-Page (HP) phase transition. In particular, we study the relation between the minimum temperature and the HP phase transition temperature for static, spherically symmetric AdS black holes in pure Lovelock gravity. For the electromagnetically neutral case in Einstein gravity, the minimum temperature in $(d+1)$ dimensions coincides with the HP transition temperature in $d$ dimensions, while in higher pure Lovelock theories this relation is modified by a dimension- and order-dependent factor, reducing to the Einstein result in appropriate limits. For charged AdS black holes, in the grand canonical ensemble, in general relativity, the two temperatures differ by a simple dimension-dependent factor, whereas no universal relation persists in higher curvature pure Lovelock theories. We further analyze the normalized Ruppeiner scalar curvature at the HP transition and show that it is a universal constant depending only on the spacetime dimension for electromagnetically neutral black holes in pure Lovelock theories. The normalized scalar curvature remains a constant, under appropriate conditions, even for the charged static spherically symmetric black holes in the grand canonical ensemble for the Einstein theory case, whereas in general pure Lovelock theories it depends on thermodynamic parameters such as pressure and electrostatic potential, asymptotically approaching a constant in the large-pressure or simultaneous large-potential and large-pressure limits.

</details>


### [22] [Gravitational superfluorescence from superradiant axion clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10776)
*Zhen-Hong Lyu,Rong-Gen Cai,Jing Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Boson clouds around spinning black holes can undergo a self-amplified quadrupolar transition via gravitational-wave feedback, producing a delayed, coherent burst analogous to superfluorescence; this effect is modeled by an effective two-level system and may be detectable with future gravitational-wave observatories.

围绕旋转黑洞的玻色子云可通过引力波反馈引发自放大四极跃迁，产生延迟且相干的爆发信号，类似于超荧光（superfluorescence）；该效应通过一个有效的二能级系统建模，并可能被未来的引力波探测器观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper seeks to explore novel observational signatures from boson clouds formed through superradiance around spinning black holes, specifically focusing on nonlinear collective effects that could generate distinctive gravitational-wave signals. Traditional analyses often assume linear or perturbative dynamics, missing potential cooperative phenomena akin to quantum optical effects like superfluorescence.
\n\n
本文旨在探索通过超辐射（superradiance）在旋转黑洞周围形成的玻色子云所产生的新型观测信号，特别关注可能产生独特引力波信号的非线性集体效应。传统分析通常假设线性或微扰动力学，忽略了类似于量子光学中超荧光（superfluorescence）等合作现象。

Method: The authors construct an effective two-level model for the boson cloud, treating the dominant superradiant mode and a quadrupolar decay channel as coupled states. Gravitational radiation reaction provides feedback that stimulates further transitions, leading to a logistic growth in population inversion. This analytical framework is validated against full numerical simulations of the Einstein–Klein–Gordon system.
\n\n
作者构建了一个玻色子云的有效二能级模型，将主导的超辐射模式与一个四极衰变通道视为耦合态。引力辐射反作用（radiation reaction）提供反馈，刺激进一步跃迁，导致粒子数反转呈逻辑斯蒂增长（logistic growth）。该解析框架通过完整的Einstein–Klein–Gordon系统数值模拟进行了验证。

Result: The model predicts a delayed, intense burst of gravitational waves with a characteristic envelope shaped by the avalanche dynamics. The peak strain scales favorably with detector sensitivity, suggesting that next-generation observatories (e.g., LISA, Einstein Telescope) could detect such signals from nearby massive black holes hosting ultralight boson clouds.
\n\n
该模型预测了一种由雪崩动力学塑造的、具有特征包络的延迟强引力波爆发。其峰值应变与探测器灵敏度呈有利标度关系，表明下一代观测设备（如LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜）可能探测到来自邻近大质量黑洞周围的超轻玻色子云所产生的此类信号。

Conclusion: This work identifies a new cooperative emission mechanism—gravitational superfluorescence—in boson clouds around black holes, extending the phenomenology of superradiant instabilities beyond linear theory. It provides a concrete, detectable signature for ultralight particles in the dark sector, enhancing the scientific case for future gravitational-wave missions.
\n\n
本工作在黑洞周围的玻色子云中识别出一种新的合作辐射机制——引力超荧光（gravitational superfluorescence），将超辐射不稳定性现象学拓展至线性理论之外。它为暗区中的超轻粒子提供了一个具体且可探测的信号，增强了未来引力波任务的科学依据。

Abstract: Boson clouds formed via superradiance around spinning black holes offer a novel gravitational-wave probe for weakly interacting ultralight particles. We show that such gravitational atoms can undergo a self-stimulated avalanche: a coherent quadrupolar transition is seeded and then amplified by gravitational radiation feedback. We formulate an effective two-level description, validated by numerical simulations, that captures the logistic population transfer and the resulting delayed gravitational-wave pulse with a characteristic envelope, and assess its detectability with future detectors. As a gravitational analogue of superfluorescence, this cooperative emission mechanism opens a new observational avenue into the ultralight dark sector.

</details>


### [23] [Black hole formation by a scalar field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10792)
*D. E. Afanasev,M. O. Katanaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper presents a Liouville solution in General Relativity coupled to an exponential scalar field potential unbounded from below. This time-dependent, Lorentz-invariant solution describes the dynamical formation of a spherically symmetric black hole whose event horizon emerges at a finite time with zero radius and expands at light speed. The associated geometric defect resembles that of a spacetime monopole or spherical dislocation, and comparison with Schwarzschild geometry gives a mass function $M(t) \propto t^2$.
\n\n
本文提出了一种在具有无下界的指数标量场势的广义相对论中的Liouville解。该解具有时间依赖性且对全局Lorentz变换不变，描述了一个球对称黑洞的动力学形成过程：事件视界在有限时刻以零半径出现，并以光速向外膨胀至无穷远。相关的几何缺陷类似于时空单极子或球形位错所产生的缺陷，与Schwarzschild解对比可得质量函数 $M(t) \propto t^2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore exact solutions in General Relativity with scalar fields that exhibit nontrivial time dependence yet retain global symmetries like Lorentz invariance. Specifically, the authors aim to understand how black holes can form dynamically from scalar field configurations with exponential potentials unbounded from below—a setting relevant to certain cosmological or gravitational collapse scenarios beyond static vacuum solutions like Schwarzschild.
\n\n
动机在于探索广义相对论中标量场存在下的精确解，这些解虽具有非平凡的时间依赖性但仍保持全局对称性（如Lorentz不变性）。作者特别关注由无下界的指数势标量场构型所导致的黑洞动力学形成过程——这一情形超越了Schwarzschild等静态真空解，对某些宇宙学或引力坍缩场景具有重要意义。

Method: The authors analyze the Einstein-scalar field equations with an exponential potential $V(\phi) \propto -e^{\lambda\phi}$ (unbounded from below). They employ the Liouville ansatz—a specific metric-scalar field configuration known to yield integrable systems—and impose spherical symmetry and global Lorentz invariance. By solving the resulting field equations, they construct an explicit time-dependent solution and examine its causal structure, horizon dynamics, and asymptotic geometry. Comparison with the Schwarzschild metric is used to extract an effective time-dependent mass function.
\n\n
作者分析具有指数势 $V(\phi) \propto -e^{\lambda\phi}$（无下界）的Einstein-标量场方程。他们采用Liouville ansatz——一种已知可导出可积系统的特定度规-标量场构型——并施加球对称性和全局Lorentz不变性。通过求解所得场方程，构造出一个显式的时间依赖解，并研究其因果结构、视界动力学和渐近几何。通过与Schwarzschild度规比较，提取出一个有效的时间依赖质量函数。

Result: The solution describes a spherically symmetric black hole forming at a finite time $t = t_0$, with an event horizon that originates as a point ($r=0$) and expands radially at the speed of light: $r_h(t) = c(t - t_0)$. The spacetime exhibits a conical-like geometric defect at the origin, analogous to that of a monopole or spherical dislocation. The effective gravitational mass inferred from asymptotic comparison with Schwarzschild geometry grows quadratically in time: $M(t) \propto (t - t_0)^2$.
\n\n
该解描述了一个在有限时刻 $t = t_0$ 形成的球对称黑洞，其事件视界从一点（$r=0$）开始，并以光速径向膨胀：$r_h(t) = c(t - t_0)$。时空在原点处表现出类似锥形的几何缺陷，类似于单极子或球形位错所产生的缺陷。通过与Schwarzschild几何的渐近比较所推断的有效引力质量随时间平方增长：$M(t) \propto (t - t_0)^2$。

Conclusion: The Liouville solution provides an exact, time-dependent model of black hole formation driven by a scalar field with an unbounded exponential potential. It demonstrates that Lorentz-invariant, non-static black hole geometries can exist and exhibit unique features such as light-speed horizon expansion and a topological/geometric defect at formation. The quadratic time dependence of mass contrasts sharply with stationary black holes and suggests novel mechanisms for gravitational collapse in scalar-tensor theories.
\n\n
Liouville解提供了一个由无下界指数势标量场驱动的黑洞形成的精确、时间依赖模型。它表明存在满足Lorentz不变性的非静态黑洞几何，具有诸如视界以光速膨胀及在形成点出现拓扑/几何缺陷等独特特征。质量对时间的二次依赖性与静态黑洞形成鲜明对比，暗示了标量-张量理论中引力坍缩的新机制。

Abstract: The Liouville solution in General Relativity with a scalar field is discussed. This solution is invariant with respect to global Lorentz transformations, and dependence on time cannot be removed. If the scalar field potential is exponential and unbounded from below, the Liouville solution describes the formation of spherically symmetric black hole. The event horizon is a sphere, which appears with infinitesimal radius at a finite moment of time and afterwards expands with the velocity of light to infinity. A distant observer can measure the geometric defect at the point where the horizon appears. It is similar to the defect produced by the monopole or spherical dislocation of space-time. Comparison with the Schwarzschild solution yields the mass function which is proportional to the time squared.

</details>


### [24] [Convex foliations and trapped submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10910)
*Gustavo Dotti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper investigates whether compact trapped submanifolds (CTMs) of codimension >1 can exist outside black hole horizons, testing this in symmetrically collapsing $(n+1)$-dimensional spacetimes ($n \geq 2$) and the full sub-extremal Kerr-Newman family. It finds they cannot, supporting the view that lower-dimensional CTMs (e.g., trapped loops) are intrinsic black hole signatures.
\n\n
本文研究了余维数大于1的紧致俘获子流形（Compact Trapped Submanifolds, CTMs）能否存在于黑洞视界之外，在对称坍缩的$(n+1)$维时空（$n \geq 2$）和整个次极端Kerr-Newman黑洞族中进行了检验。结果表明它们不能存在，从而支持了低维CTMs（如俘获环）应被视为黑洞特征的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from a conjecture in general relativity asserting that compact trapped submanifolds (CTMs) with codimension greater than one cannot intersect the domain of outer communications—i.e., the region outside the event horizon—of a black hole spacetime. Validating this conjecture would clarify the geometric nature of black hole boundaries and support the interpretation of lower-dimensional trapped structures as genuine indicators of strong gravity regions.
\n\n
动机源于广义相对论中的一个猜想：余维数大于1的紧致俘获子流形（CTMs）不能与黑洞时空的外通信域（即事件视界外部区域）相交。验证该猜想有助于澄清黑洞边界的几何本质，并支持将低维俘获结构视为强引力区域真实指标的解释。

Method: The authors analyze two classes of spacetimes: (1) spherically or symmetrically collapsing spacetimes in $n+1$ dimensions with $n \geq 2$, where explicit geometric control is possible; and (2) the entire sub-extremal Kerr-Newman family in 3+1 dimensions, which includes rotating and charged black holes. They examine the existence and location of compact trapped submanifolds of codimension $>1$ (e.g., trapped curves or loops) by studying null geodesic congruences and the sign of expansions associated with higher-codimensional surfaces, leveraging the Raychaudhuri equation and properties of the stress-energy tensor satisfying energy conditions.
\n\n
作者分析了两类时空：(1) $n+1$维（$n \geq 2$）中球对称或对称坍缩时空，其中可进行显式几何控制；(2) 3+1维中整个次极端Kerr-Newman族，包含旋转和带电黑洞。他们通过研究零测地线汇及其与高余维曲面相关的膨胀标量符号，考察余维数$>1$的紧致俘获子流形（如俘获曲线或环）的存在性与位置，利用Raychaudhuri方程以及满足能量条件的应力-能量张量的性质。

Result: In both symmetrically collapsing spacetimes and all sub-extremal Kerr-Newman black holes, no compact trapped submanifolds of codimension greater than one are found to intersect the domain of outer communications. This provides strong evidence that such CTMs must lie strictly inside the event horizon, reinforcing their role as exclusive signatures of black hole interiors.
\n\n
在对称坍缩时空和所有次极端Kerr-Newman黑洞中，均未发现余维数大于1的紧致俘获子流形（CTMs）与外通信域相交。这为这类CTMs必须严格位于事件视界内部提供了有力证据，强化了其作为黑洞内部独有特征的角色。

Conclusion: The results support the conjecture that compact trapped submanifolds of codimension $>1$ cannot exist outside black hole horizons. Consequently, lower-dimensional trapped structures—such as trapped loops—are not artifacts of coordinate choices or external geometry but robust, invariant features signaling the presence of a black hole, thereby offering a refined geometric criterion for black hole identification beyond traditional marginally outer trapped surfaces (MOTS).

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing high-energy physics searches rely heavily on colliders, astrophysical observations, and gravitational wave detectors. Atomic interferometry offers a highly sensitive terrestrial platform to detect coherent oscillations of such fields through their influence on atomic quantum phases, thereby probing parameter space inaccessible to conventional techniques.\n\n
当前高能物理探索严重依赖对撞机、天体物理观测和引力波探测器。然而，在超对称（SUSY）和超引力（SUGRA）扩展中预言的轻隐藏扇区场——尤其是超轻模场或胀子——可能因其与标准模型的耦合极弱而逃逸这些探测手段。原子干涉仪提供了一种高灵敏度的地面平台，可通过这些场对原子量子相位的影响探测其相干振荡，从而探索传统技术无法触及的参数空间。

Method: The authors construct an effective coupling between ultralight hidden scalars and Standard Model fields by expanding fundamental SUSY/SUGRA Lagrangian parameters—such as the gauge kinetic function $f_a$, Kähler metric $K_{i\\bar{j}}$, superpotential Yukawa couplings $Y^{ijk}$, Higgs soft masses $m_{H_u}^2, m_{H_d}^2$, and the QCD scale $\\Lambda_{\\text{QCD}}$—along the direction of a hidden modulus $\\phi$. They relate time-varying shifts in these parameters to an oscillating atomic phase $\\Delta \\Phi(t)$ measurable in long-baseline atom interferometers like MAGIS/AION. The mapping is derived via:\n$$\n\\frac{\\delta \\mathcal{L}_{\\text{SM}}}{\\mathcal{L}_{\\text{SM}}} \\supset \\sum_I c_I \\frac{\\dot{\\phi}}{M_{\\text{Pl}}} \\mathcal{O}_I,\n$$\nwhere $c_I$ encode derivatives like $\\partial \\ln f_a / \\partial \\phi$, and $\\mathcal{O}_I$ are SM operators affecting atomic energy levels. The resulting phase shift scales as $\\Delta \\Phi \\propto c_I \\cdot (\\phi_0 / M_{\\text{Pl}}) \\cdot T^2$, with $T$ the interferometer baseline time.\n\n
作者通过沿隐藏模场 $\\phi$ 方向展开 SUSY/SUGRA 拉格朗日量中的基本参数——如规范动能函数 $f_a$、Kähler 度量 $K_{i\\bar{j}}$、超势 Yukawa 耦合 $Y^{ijk}$、Higgs 软质量 $m_{H_u}^2, m_{H_d}^2$ 和 QCD 能标 $\\Lambda_{\\text{QCD}}$——构建了超轻隐藏标量与标准模型场之间的有效耦合。他们将这些参数的时间变化与 MAGIS/AION 等长基线原子干涉仪中可测的振荡原子相位 $\\Delta \\Phi(t)$ 关联起来。该映射通过以下形式导出：\n$$\n\\frac{\\delta \\mathcal{L}_{\\text{SM}}}{\\mathcal{L}_{\\text{SM}}} \\supset \\sum_I c_I \\frac{\\dot{\\phi}}{M_{\\text{Pl}}} \\mathcal{O}_I,\n$$\n其中 $c_I$ 编码了诸如 $\\partial \\ln f_a / \\partial \\phi$ 等导数，$\\mathcal{O}_I$ 是影响原子能级的标准模型算符。所得相移按 $\\Delta \\Phi \\propto c_I \\cdot (\\phi_0 / M_{\\text{Pl}}) \\cdot T^2$ 缩放，$T$ 为干涉仪基线时间。

Result: The paper establishes a quantitative link between atomic interferometric phase measurements and fundamental SUSY/SUGRA structures. It shows that future MAGIS-100 and AION-km experiments, with projected phase sensitivities of $\\delta \\Phi \\sim 10^{-5} - 10^{-6}$ rad, could constrain visible-hidden mixing angles down to $\\epsilon \\sim 10^{-15} - 10^{-17}$, corresponding to Planck-suppressed couplings with small admixtures. Conclusion: Long-baseline atom interferometers such as MAGIS and AION constitute a novel class of precision experiments capable of probing ultralight hidden-sector scalars motivated by supersymmetry and string theory. MAGIS 和 AION 等长基线原子干涉仪构成了一类新型精密实验，能够探测由超对称和弦论所启发的超轻隐藏扇区标量。通过将测得的原子相移与基本 SUSY 参数的导数关联，这类装置提供了通往原本不可及的红外物理的直接通道，是对新物理的对撞机、天体物理和引力波探针的重要补充。

Abstract: Atomic interferometry provides a sensitive near Earth probe of high energy physics through precision measurements of quantum phase. In this Letter, we point out that MAGIS and AION like long-baseline atom interferometers can also be used to test supersymmetric hidden sectors, if these sectors contain ultralight moduli, dilatons or hidden scalars that induce coherent phase oscillations. In such a setup, the measured atomic phase does not only constrain an effective phenomenological scalar coupling. It can be related to derivatives of supersymmetric gauge kinetic functions, Kähler metrics, Yukawa couplings, Higgs sector parameters and the QCD scale along light hidden sector directions. We derive the mapping from a generic SUSY/SUGRA modulus to the effective atom interferometric coupling, and show that future phase sensitivities may probe very small visible sector admixtures of otherwise hidden fields. This identifies MAGIS/AION type experiments as non-collider probes of supersymmetric and string-motivated infrared relics, complementary to gravitational wave, astrophysical and collider searches.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors exploit the inverse Gertsenshtein effect, wherein HFGWs propagating through astrophysical magnetic fields convert into electromagnetic radiation (radio photons). They estimate the expected radio signal from two classes of HFGW sources: (1) transient bursts from PBH mergers, and (2) continuous monochromatic emission from ultralight boson clouds around spinning PBHs formed via superradiance. 作者利用逆Gertsenshtein效应，即高频引力波（HFGWs）在穿过天体物理磁场时会转换为电磁辐射（射电光子）。他们估算了两类HFGW源所产生的射电信号：(1) 来自原初黑洞（PBH）并合的瞬态爆发；(2) 来自通过超辐射机制在旋转PBH周围形成的超轻玻色子云的连续单色辐射。通过对转换概率建模，并与仪器噪声及背景进行比较，计算了现有射电望远镜（CHIME和FAST）的灵敏度预期。

Conclusion: Existing radio telescopes represent a powerful and underutilized avenue for detecting high-frequency gravitational waves via photon conversion in magnetic fields. Abstract: High-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) in the MHz to GHz regime can convert into radio photons in the presence of astrophysical magnetic fields through the inverse Gertsenshtein effect. We show that existing radio telescopes like CHIME and FAST are excellent tools for detecting HFGW sources, significantly outperforming many existing experiments at detecting primordial black hole (PBH) mergers, the most realistic sources of transient HFGWs.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the possibility that dark matter may be composite rather than elementary, which relaxes the canonical thermal unitarity bound that typically limits thermal relic dark matter to masses below $\mathcal{O}(100)$ TeV. Existing high-energy gamma-ray observatories now provide sensitivity in this uncharted mass regime, making it timely to conduct a systematic, model-independent analysis.

Method: The authors perform a model-agnostic indirect detection analysis by combining gamma-ray flux measurements and upper limits from five major air-shower experiments: Tibet AS$_γ$, LHAASO, KASCADE-Grande, Pierre Auger Observatory, and Telescope Array. They assume dark matter annihilates into standard model particles and compute the resulting extragalactic diffuse gamma-ray flux, accounting for cosmological propagation and absorption (e.g., by the extragalactic background light). Using these datasets, they derive upper limits on the velocity-averaged annihilation cross section $\langle \sigma v \rangle$ across the mass range $10^5$--$10^{12}$ GeV without assuming a specific particle physics model.
\n\n
作者通过整合五个主要空气簇射实验（Tibet AS$_γ$、LHAASO、KASCADE-Grande、Pierre Auger Observatory 和 Telescope Array）的伽马射线流量测量值与上限，开展了一项与模型无关的间接探测分析。他们假设暗物质湮灭为标准模型粒子，并计算由此产生的河外弥漫伽马射线流量，同时考虑宇宙学传播效应及吸收过程（例如被河外背景光吸收）。利用这些数据集，他们在 $10^5$--$10^{12}$ GeV 质量范围内推导出速度平均湮灭截面 $\langle \sigma v \rangle$ 的上限，且不依赖于特定的粒子物理模型。

Result: The analysis yields world-leading constraints on the dark matter annihilation cross section $\langle \sigma v \rangle$ for masses between $10^5$ and $10^{12}$ GeV—significantly surpassing previous limits from Fermi-LAT, H.E.S.S., MAGIC, and other instruments in this ultra-high-mass regime. Conclusion: High-energy gamma-ray observations from ground-based air shower arrays are a powerful and underutilized probe of ultra-heavy dark matter. By combining multi-experiment data in a model-independent framework, this work establishes stringent new limits on annihilating dark matter beyond the electroweak scale and highlights the potential for future discoveries as detectors like LHAASO accumulate more exposure. Conclusion: The Algebra of Physical Space (APS) provides a powerful, geometrically intuitive, and computationally streamlined framework for reformulating key aspects of the Constructive Standard Model (CSM), particularly for massive spin-1/2 fields. Abstract: Following previous work, the Algebra of Physical Space (APS) is used to explore Wigner-covariance and spin/helicity fields within the Constructive Standard Model (CSM) of Particle Physics. The spinor formalism of the APS is used to derive explicit Wigner-covariance of Lorentz spinors, and equivalencies with the CSM are demonstrated via the Scattering Algebra (SA). Constructive fields for spin-1/2 and spin-1 are given in the APS and the necessary maps for Wigner-covariance are proposed. The forms of these spin fields are equivalent to Pauli spinors, thereby serving as a new bridge between the CSM and the study of Quantum Information. It is further seen that spin-1/2 fields of the APS are equivalent to the spin-1/2 fields in the CSM, but the massless cases cannot yet be handled within the SA due to various complications. Similarly, while spin-1 fields exist inside the APS, their SA equivalents deviate from the originally proposed spin-1 fields of the CSM; so further work is needed to prove correspondence between spin-1 fields of the APS and the CSM. Sample Lagrangian densities of the CSM are analyzed using the methods herein, are given geometric interpretations, and are connected to traditional Pauli Theory. Finally, the hff (Higgs and two massive fermions) Lagrangian density is used to determine the first constructive scattering amplitude that is defined purely in terms of the APS. Concerningly, this leads to an anti-Hermitian action term, and this surprise is later confirmed using traditional CSM techniques. TL;DR: This paper solves the Goldstone-boson-exchange (GBE) relativistic constituent quark model for light and strange baryons using the Faddeev approach, reproducing their vacuum mass spectrum below 2 GeV. The study also estimates consequences for ideal-gas baryon yields and ratios, showing that absolute yields respond to ~10 MeV mass shifts while ratios change significantly only when baryons have differing quark-mass dependencies.\n\n
本文采用Faddeev方法求解Goldstone-boson-exchange (GBE) 相对论组分夸克模型，成功复现了2 GeV以下轻味与奇异重子的真空质量谱。通过参数化研究，考察了组分夸克质量、交换玻色子质量、禁闭强度及夸克-介子耦合常数在介质中可能的变化对重子谱的影响，发现重子谱对夸克-介子耦合常数最为敏感。文中还定性估计了这些质量移动对理想气体重子产额及其比值的影响：绝对产额在质量移动仅几10 MeV时即显著变化，而产额比值仅当所比较的重子具有不同的组分夸克质量依赖性时才发生明显改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how the properties of light and strange baryons, as described by the Goldstone-boson-exchange relativistic constituent quark model, respond to modifications induced by a dense or hot medium—such as those encountered in heavy-ion collisions or neutron stars. This requires assessing the sensitivity of the baryon mass spectrum to changes in fundamental model parameters like constituent quark masses, exchange boson masses, confinement strength, and the quark-meson coupling constant.\n\n
动机在于理解Goldstone-boson-exchange相对论组分夸克模型所描述的轻味与奇异重子性质如何响应致密或高温介质（如重离子碰撞或中子星内部）诱导的修正。这需要评估重子质量谱对组分夸克质量、交换玻色子质量、禁闭强度及夸克-介子耦合常数等基本模型参数变化的敏感性。

Method: The authors solve the relativistic three-body problem for baryons within the Goldstone-boson-exchange (GBE) constituent quark model using the Faddeev formalism. They then perform a parametric study where key model parameters—the constituent quark mass $m_q$, the Goldstone boson mass $m_B$, the confinement scale $\sigma$, and the quark-meson coupling constant $g$—are varied according to power-law scaling relations, some inspired by current algebra at the constituent quark level. The resulting shifts in baryon masses are computed, and their implications for thermal hadron yields in an ideal gas are estimated via Boltzmann statistics: $Y \propto e^{-M/T}$.\n\n
作者在Goldstone-boson-exchange (GBE) 组分夸克模型框架下，利用Faddeev形式求解重子的相对论三体问题。随后进行参数化研究，将关键模型参数——组分夸克质量 $m_q$、Goldstone玻色子质量 $m_B$、禁闭尺度 $\sigma$ 和夸克-介子耦合常数 $g$——按照幂律标度关系进行变化，其中部分关系受组分夸克层次的流代数启发。计算由此产生的重子质量移动，并通过玻尔兹曼统计 $Y \\propto e^{-M/T}$ 定性估计其对理想气体热强子产额的影响。

Result: The baryon mass spectrum below 2 GeV is well reproduced in vacuum. Under medium-modified parameter variations, the spectrum shows greatest sensitivity to the quark-meson coupling constant $g$. Generally, baryon masses decrease as the constituent quark mass $m_q$ decreases. For thermal yields, absolute abundances change appreciably for mass shifts of just a few 10 MeV due to the exponential dependence $e^{-M/T}$. However, yield ratios (e.g., $\Lambda/p$) are robust unless the involved baryons exhibit different functional dependencies on $m_q$.\n\n
在真空中，2 GeV以下的重子质量谱被很好地复现。在介质修正的参数变化下，谱对夸克-介子耦合常数 $g$ 最为敏感。通常，随着组分夸克质量 $m_q$ 减小，重子质量下降。对于热产额，由于指数依赖 $e^{-M/T}$，仅几10 MeV的质量移动即可显著改变绝对丰度。然而，产额比值（如 $\Lambda/p$）具有鲁棒性，除非所涉重子对 $m_q$ 具有不同的函数依赖关系。

Conclusion: The GBE constituent quark model, solved via the Faddeev approach, provides a consistent description of the light and strange baryon spectrum. Medium effects modeled through parameter scaling reveal that baryon masses are most responsive to changes in the quark-meson coupling. This has potentially observable consequences for particle production in heavy-ion collisions, particularly for absolute yields, while yield ratios serve as more stable probes unless specific quark-content-dependent mass shifts occur.\n\n
通过Faddeev方法求解的GBE组分夸克模型为轻味与奇异重子谱提供了自洽描述。通过参数标度模拟的介质效应表明，重子质量对夸克-介子耦合的变化最为敏感。这对重离子碰撞中的粒子产额具有潜在可观测影响，尤其体现在绝对产额上；而产额比值作为更稳定的探针，仅在出现依赖夸克组分的质量移动时才会显著变化。

Abstract: We solve the Goldstone-boson-exchange (GBE) relativistic constituent quark model of light and strange baryons by using the Faddeev approach. The model reproduces the vacuum mass spectrum of light and strange baryons below $2$ GeV reasonably well. To test the sensitivity of the model to possible medium-induced effects, we vary the masses of the constituent quarks and the exchange bosons, the confinement strength, and the quark-meson coupling constant. In a parametric study, we consider a set of power law scaling relations for these parameters, including some motivated by constituent quark level current algebra relations. We find that the baryon spectrum is most sensitive to the quark-meson coupling constant, and generally observe a decrease in baryon mass with decreasing constituent quark mass. We qualitatively estimate the impact of these mass shifts on ideal-gas baryon yields and yield ratios. Absolute yields can change significantly already for mass shifts of a few $10$~MeV, whereas yield ratios are strongly modified only when the compared baryons have different constituent-quark-mass dependence.

Abstract: We investigate a harmonic interpretation of the hidden flavor coordinates (Q,G,C) introduced previously in a low-rank ternary description of the fermion mass spectrum. Combining the fermionic harmonic modes naturally generates the even sequence (2,4,6,8,10), which exhibits a suggestive correspondence with the electroweak and Higgs scales. While this correspondence remains speculative, it points toward a common harmonic structure underlying both fermionic and bosonic states. Although the framework is exploratory, it indicates that fermion masses, flavor organization, and bosonic excitations may represent different manifestations of a deeper hidden harmonic structure.
