  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We establish the general functional form of the energy-momentum-tensor two-point function in Euclidean coordinate space at zero and finite temperature.   |κ_g| &\in [0.70,\, 1.30] \quad \text{at 95\% CL},. \end{align*} These results are in excellent agreement with the official ATLAS and CMS combinations.

Conclusion: Machine learning can uncover hidden geometric structures in the space of Feynman integrals, enabling dramatically more efficient IBP reductions. Abstract: In this paper, we use machine learning to discover a new seeding strategy for integration-by-parts reduction of Feynman integrals, which is a frequent bottleneck in state-of-the-art calculations in theoretical particle and gravitational-wave physics. Our strategy allows us to reduce multi-loop integrals with large numerator powers via essentially the standard Laporta algorithm but with a sparse selection of seed integrals that grows only linearly with the numerator power, whereas existing strategies lead to growth with a polynomial power that increases with the complexity of the integral being reduced. Abstract: The hypothesis of a violation of isospin-symmetry in pp and AA collisions is discussed. Abstract: We present DarkAgents: a multi-agent system that leverages the reasoning and code-generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs), together with deterministic tested human-written code, to build orchestrated pipelines for theoretical astroparticle physics research. While related approaches have been proposed in collider physics and cosmology, DarkAgents targets the specific challenges of this domain, such as model building, complex pipeline computations, multiple constraints and assumption auditing. DarkAgent-PT provides as output i) the best-fit values of model parameters, ii) their existing experimental and observational constraints, iii) an audit report of the assumptions and priors entering both i) and ii), of particular relevance for astroparticle physics. Our test runs identify inconsistencies in some fits in the literature and produce novel ones based on the dissipative bulk-flow GW template. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/PhysicsZandi/DarkAgents.

### [44] [Does the Weinberg angle allow a local hidden-variable description for the leptonic decays of an entangled $ZZ$ pair?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10737)
*Junle Pei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether quantum entanglement in $Z_1Z_2$ diboson systems produced from a spin-0 scalar decay can be mimicked by a local hidden-variable theory (LHVT) under angular-momentum conservation. In the physical case of a scalar coupling $sZ^\mu Z_\mu$, LHVT is viable only exactly at production threshold ($m_s = 2m_Z$), not above it.

本文研究了由自旋为0的标量粒子衰变产生的$Z_1Z_2$双玻色子系统中的量子纠缠是否能在角动量守恒约束下被局域隐变量理论（LHVT）所复现。通过匹配轻子衰变道$Z_1(\to e^-_1 e^+_1)Z_2(\to e^-_2 e^+_2)$中的角分布，作者推导出LHVT存在的精确条件。他们发现，对于$w \neq 0$的情形，除平凡的直积态外，仅有一个特定的纠缠纯态（对应$a_1=a_3=-a_2=1/\sqrt{3}$且$b_2=b_3=0$）在弱混合角$θ_W$的受限窗口内允许LHVT描述；而对于$w = 0$的情形，他们建立了完整的代数判据。在物理上相关的标量耦合$sZ^\mu Z_\mu$情形中，LHVT仅在产生阈值$m_s = 2m_Z$处成立，而在$m_s > 2m_Z$时不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the foundational question of whether quantum correlations observed in high-energy particle physics—specifically in entangled diboson systems—can be explained by classical local hidden-variable theories respecting fundamental symmetries like angular-momentum conservation. While Bell inequalities provide a general test for nonlocality, this work seeks a more refined analysis: determining exact conditions under which a local model can reproduce the full angular distribution predicted by quantum field theory (QFT), rather than just violating a single inequality.
\n\n
该工作的动机源于一个基础性问题：高能粒子物理中观测到的量子关联（特别是纠缠双玻色子系统中的关联）是否能被遵守基本对称性（如角动量守恒）的经典局域隐变量理论所解释。尽管贝尔不等式为非定域性提供了普适检验，但本文旨在进行更精细的分析：确定局域模型在何种精确条件下能够复现量子场论（QFT）所预言的完整角分布，而非仅仅检验单一不等式的破坏。

Method: The authors parameterize the spin-density matrix of the $Z_1Z_2$ pair using coefficients $a_i, b_j$ derived from the underlying QFT amplitude for scalar decay. They construct a general local hidden-variable theory (LHVT) ansatz for the joint angular distribution of the leptonic decay products, constrained by angular-momentum conservation and rotational invariance. By equating the LHVT angular distribution term-by-term with the QFT prediction, they derive a system of algebraic equations and positivity constraints on the hidden-variable probability measure. Solving these yields necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of an LHVT construction, analyzed separately for the cases $w \neq 0$ and $w = 0$, where $w$ is a kinematic parameter related to the scalar mass.

作者利用标量粒子衰变的量子场论振幅导出的系数$a_i, b_j$来参数化$Z_1Z_2$对的自旋密度矩阵。他们构建了一个满足角动量守恒和旋转不变性的局域隐变量理论（LHVT）的联合角分布一般形式。通过将LHVT角分布与QFT预言逐项匹配，推导出关于隐变量概率测度的一组代数方程和正定性约束。求解这些方程得到LHVT构造存在性的充要条件，并针对运动学参数$w$（与标量质量相关）的两种情形$w \neq 0$和$w = 0$分别进行分析。

Result: For $w \neq 0$, the only nontrivial entangled pure state admitting an LHVT description is uniquely specified by $a_1 = a_3 = -a_2 = 1/\sqrt{3}$ and $b_2 = b_3 = 0$, and this is only possible within a narrow window of the weak-mixing angle $θ_W$. For $w = 0$, the authors derive a complete set of algebraic and positivity conditions that are both necessary and sufficient for LHVT compatibility. Applying this to the physical interaction $sZ^\mu Z_\mu$, they show that at the production threshold ($m_s = 2m_Z$), $w = 0$ and the conditions are satisfied, allowing an LHVT; however, for $m_s > 2m_Z$ (i.e., $w \neq 0$), no LHVT exists except possibly for the unique state, which is not realized in this interaction.
\n\n
对于$w \neq 0$的情形，唯一允许LHVT描述的非平庸纠缠纯态由$a_1 = a_3 = -a_2 = 1/\sqrt{3}$和$b_2 = b_3 = 0$唯一确定，且仅在弱混合角$θ_W$的一个狭窄窗口内成立。对于$w = 0$的情形，作者推导出一组完整且充要的代数与正定性条件。将此应用于物理相互作用$sZ^\mu Z_\mu$时，他们证明在产生阈值（$m_s = 2m_Z$）处，$w = 0$且条件满足，LHVT存在；然而当$m_s > 2m_Z$（即$w \neq 0$）时，除上述唯一态外不存在LHVT，而该唯一态在此相互作用中并未实现。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that quantum entanglement in diboson systems from scalar decays generically cannot be described by local hidden-variable theories respecting angular-momentum conservation, except in highly fine-tuned or kinematically restricted cases (e.g., at threshold). This reinforces the genuinely quantum nature of such correlations in high-energy processes and provides a concrete framework for testing quantum foundations in collider experiments beyond standard Bell tests.

该研究表明，来自标量衰变的双玻色子系统中的量子纠缠通常无法用遵守角动量守恒的局域隐变量理论描述，除非在高度微调或运动学受限的情形下（例如在阈值处）。这强化了高能过程中此类关联的真正量子本质，并为在对撞机实验中超越标准贝尔检验、检验量子力学基础提供了具体框架。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement in diboson systems offers a useful testing ground for exploring the boundary between quantum-mechanical correlations and classical descriptions based on local hidden variables. In this work, we study the spin-polarization state of a $Z_1Z_2$ pair produced from the decay of a spin-0 particle and investigate whether the angular correlations predicted by quantum field theory (QFT) in the leptonic decays $Z_1(\to e^-_1 e^+_1)Z_2(\to e^-_2 e^+_2)$ can be reproduced by a local hidden-variable theory (LHVT) under angular-momentum conservation. By matching the LHVT angular distribution to the QFT prediction coefficient by coefficient, we derive the conditions under which an LHVT construction exists. For the case $w\neq 0$, we show that, apart from trivial product-state configurations, an LHVT construction exists only for a unique entangled pure state, corresponding to $a_1=a_3=-a_2=1/\sqrt{3}$ and $b_2=b_3=0$, together with a restricted window of the weak-mixing angle $θ_W$. For $w=0$, we derive a necessary and sufficient criterion for the existence of an LHVT construction in terms of a closed set of algebraic and positivity conditions. As an application, we consider the phenomenologically relevant interaction $sZ^μZ_μ$ and show that an LHVT construction exists at threshold $m_s=2m_Z$, whereas it does not exist for $m_s>2m_Z$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

The magnetic moments of octet baryons are then computed self-consistently from the quark-level response to the field, accounting for both valence and sea contributions.

作者采用一个统一框架，将包含介子–重子相互作用和手征对称性破缺的chiral SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型与手征组分夸克模型（$\\chi$CQM）相结合。在有限温度下，他们通过单圈真空修正显式包含Dirac sea（DS）贡献，从而在外加磁场下修正标量凝聚 $\\langle \\bar{q} q \\rangle$。八重态重子的磁矩随后通过夸克层次对磁场的响应自洽计算，同时计入价夸克和海夸克的贡献。

Result: Including the Dirac sea leads to magnetic catalysis: scalar condensates increase with magnetic field strength, resulting in a monotonic rise of octet baryon effective masses. Consequently, the magnetic moments of these baryons are significantly modified in strongly magnetized, isospin-asymmetric strange matter. The effect is more pronounced at lower temperatures and highlights the non-negligible role of vacuum polarization in such environments.

包含Dirac sea导致magnetic catalysis：标量凝聚随磁场强度增加，使得八重态重子的有效质量单调上升。因此，在强磁化且同位旋不对称的奇异物质中，这些重子的磁矩被显著修正。该效应在低温下更为显著，凸显了真空极化在此类环境中的不可忽略作用。

Conclusion: Vacuum polarization effects, particularly through Dirac sea contributions, are essential for accurately describing baryon electromagnetic structure in strong magnetic fields. This unified CQMF–$\chi$CQM approach provides a consistent theoretical foundation for studying magnetized hadronic matter relevant to compact stars and heavy-ion collision experiments.

真空极化效应，尤其是通过Dirac sea的贡献，对于准确描述强磁场中重子的电磁结构至关重要。这一统一的CQMF–$\chi$CQM方法为研究与致密星体和重离子碰撞实验相关的磁化强子物质提供了自洽的理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic moments of octet baryons in isospin asymmetric strange hadronic matter under strong external magnetic fields within a unified theoretical framework by combining the chiral SU(3) quark mean field (CQMF) model with the chiral constituent quark ($χ$CQM) model. At finite temperature, the inclusion of Dirac sea (DS) effect leads to magnetic catalysis attributing to the enhancement of scalar condensates with increasing magnetic field strength. As a consequence, the effective masses of the octet baryons exhibit a monotonic increase as a function of magnetic field. The results highlight the crucial role of vacuum polarization effects in determining the electromagnetic properties of baryons in strongly magnetized matter having relevance in heavy-ion collision and compact stars.

*Petr Baron*

TL;DR: The paper proposes a harmonic framework for fermion masses using hidden flavor coordinates $(Q,G,C)$, where the generation index $G$ maps to odd harmonics $n_G = (1,3,5)$. This yields an additive logarithmic mass spectrum and motivates a geometric flavor interpretation in the full 3D hidden-coordinate space.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The observed fermion mass hierarchy lacks a compelling theoretical explanation within the Standard Model. This paper seeks to uncover a deeper organizing principle—specifically, a harmonic structure—that could explain both the mass spectrum and flavor organization, and potentially unify fermionic and bosonic sectors.

标准模型中观测到的费米子质量等级缺乏令人信服的理论解释。先前工作引入了基于隐藏坐标 $(Q,G,C)$ 的低秩三元参数化，但其物理含义尚不明确。本文旨在揭示一种更深层的组织原理——特别是谐波结构——以解释质量谱和味结构，并可能统一费米子与玻色子扇区。

Method: The authors reinterpret the empirical fermion mass ansatz by associating the generation coordinate $G$ with odd harmonic modes $n_G = (1,3,5)$. They express the logarithm of fermion masses as an additive sum over contributions from $Q$, $G$, and $C$. They then promote the scalar combination $L = QG + C$ to a full vector $X = (Q,G,C)$ and define geometric structures—such as coordinate separations and antisymmetric invariants—in this hidden space to characterize flavor. Finally, they generate even harmonics $n_B = (2,4,6,8,10)$ by combining fermionic modes and compare them to known bosonic energy scales (e.g., electroweak and Higgs).
\n\n
作者通过将代坐标 $G$ 关联到奇次谐波模式 $n_G = (1,3,5)$，重新诠释经验性的费米子质量拟设。他们将费米子质量的对数表达为 $Q$、$G$ 和 $C$ 贡献的可加和。随后，将标量组合 $L = QG + C$ 提升为完整矢量 $X = (Q,G,C)$，并在该隐藏空间中定义几何结构（如坐标间距和反对称不变量）以刻画味。最后，通过组合费米子谐波生成偶次谐波 $n_B = (2,4,6,8,10)$，并与已知玻色子能标（如电弱与希格斯标度）进行对比。

Result: The harmonic ansatz successfully recasts the fermion mass spectrum into an additive logarithmic form, clarifying the role of each hidden coordinate. The derived bosonic harmonic sequence $n_B = (2,4,6,8,10)$ shows numerical proximity to the ratios of electroweak and Higgs mass scales, suggesting a possible common origin.

谐波拟设成功将费米子质量谱重写为可加的对数形式，阐明了每个隐藏坐标的贡献。投影量 $L = QG + C$ 被证明不足以唯一标记味态，从而验证了使用完整矢量 $X$ 的必要性。所导出的玻色子谐波序列 $n_B = (2,4,6,8,10)$ 在数值上接近电弱与希格斯质量标度的比值，暗示可能存在共同起源。

Conclusion: The paper proposes that fermion masses and flavor may emerge from a hidden three-dimensional harmonic geometry, with bosonic mass scales arising as composite even harmonics of the same underlying structure. While phenomenological and not yet embedded in a dynamical quantum field theory, this framework offers a novel geometric and harmonic perspective on the flavor puzzle and hints at a unified description of fermionic and bosonic sectors.
\n\n
本文提出，费米子质量与味结构可能源于一个隐藏的三维谐波几何，而玻色子质量标度则作为同一底层结构的复合偶次谐波出现。尽管该框架目前仍是唯象的，尚未嵌入动力学量子场论，但它为味疑难提供了新颖的几何与谐波视角，并暗示了费米子与玻色子扇区的统一描述可能性。

Abstract: We investigate a harmonic interpretation of the hidden flavor coordinates (Q,G,C) previously introduced in a low-rank ternary description of the fermion mass spectrum. The generation coordinate is associated with the odd harmonic mode sequence n_G = (1,3,5), allowing the fermion mass ansatz to be rewritten in a compact harmonic form. The corresponding logarithmic spectrum reveals an additive structure in which the hidden coordinates contribute directly to the observed mass hierarchy. Conclusion: MATCHA provides an efficient, user-friendly tool for HEFT matching that extends beyond SMEFT assumptions, enabling systematic exploration of BSM physics with non-linear Higgs sectors and facilitating consistent EFT interpretations of collider data.
\n\n
MATCHA 提供了一个高效且用户友好的 HEFT 匹配工具，突破了 SMEFT 假设的限制，使得对具有非线性 Higgs 扇区的 BSM 物理进行系统性探索成为可能，并有助于对对撞机数据进行一致的 EFT 解释。

Abstract: We present MATChing HEFT Amplitudes (MATCHA), a Mathematica package designed for leading-order (LO) matching of an ultraviolet (UV) model to the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT). MATCHA performs the matching of non-decoupling effects $\mathcal{O}(1)$ to the LO HEFT lagrangian for an arbitrary number of Higgs fields. The main benefit of \heftmatcha is that it is built on existing packages such as \texttt{FeynArts} and \texttt{FormCalc}, which are familiar to the user, and directly benefits from the established features of these packages. In addition, \heftmatcha is designed to require minimal input from the user, requiring solely the \texttt{FeynRules} output files and the desired order of expansion. In this way, \heftmatcha provides the leading low-energy couplings capturing non-decoupling effects of HEFT.

### [53] [Calling the Brane Next Door: The Kaluza-Klein Tower as a Gravitational Information Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09969)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to investigate whether gravitational interactions alone—mediated by bulk gravitons—can enable communication between two parallel brane-worlds in extra-dimensional models. \n\n

The compactification geometry determines KK masses $m_n$, wavefunctions $\psi_n(y)$, brane overlap factors $|\psi_n(y_1)\psi_n(y_2)|$, and propagation phases $e^{i k_n \cdot x}$, which together define a MIMO channel matrix $H_{ij}$. In the resolved-mode limit (where individual KK modes are spectrally distinguishable), standard information theory tools—such as channel capacity, water-filling power allocation, effective rank, and sparse coding—are applied to characterize communication potential.
\n\n
作者在具有紧致额外维度的高维时空中计算膜到膜的推迟引力子传播子，并将其分解为Kaluza-Klein模，分析从隐逝（阈值以下）到传播（阈值以上）行为的转变。基于此构建一个转移核（transfer kernel），将一个膜上的信号映射到另一个膜。紧致化几何决定了KK质量 $m_n$、波函数 $\psi_n(y)$、膜重叠因子 $|\psi_n(y_1)\psi_n(y_2)|$ 以及传播相位 $e^{i k_n \cdot x}$，这些共同定义了一个MIMO信道矩阵 $H_{ij}$。在可分辨模极限（individual KK modes spectrally distinguishable）下，应用标准信息论工具（如信道容量、注水功率分配、有效秩和稀疏编码）来刻画通信潜力。

The full channel is described by a MIMO matrix whose entries encode geometric data of the compact space. In the resolved-mode regime, the channel approximates parallel Gaussian subchannels, allowing application of classical information-theoretic results: capacity scales with the number of active modes, water-filling optimizes power distribution, and sparse occupancy codes may enhance robustness. Crucially, even without intentional signaling, the spectral and modal structure of the KK tower—observable via high-energy gravitational processes—could reveal the existence and partial geometry of a hidden brane-world.

在首个Kaluza-Klein质量阈值以下，通信是纯四维的，仅由无质量引力子介导，导致膜间联系呈隐逝性并指数压低。超过阈值后，有质量KK模变为壳上（on-shell）状态，充当独立的传播子信道。完整信道由一个MIMO矩阵描述，其元素编码了紧致空间的几何信息。在可分辨模区域，该信道近似为并行高斯子信道，可应用经典信息论结果：容量随活跃模数量增长，注水法优化功率分配，稀疏占位编码可增强鲁棒性。关键的是，即使没有有意信号，KK塔的谱与模结构——可通过高能引力过程观测——也可能揭示隐藏膜世界的存在及其部分几何。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that gravity alone can, in principle, support structured communication between neighboring brane-worlds via the Kaluza-Klein tower of massive gravitons. Abstract: Could two worlds localised on neighbouring branes communicate through gravity alone? We investigate this question in a minimal higher-dimensional framework in which Standard Model fields are confined to our brane while gravity propagates through the bulk. From the brane-to-brane graviton propagator we derive the retarded transfer kernel of the inter-brane link and identify the transition from evanescent to propagating Kaluza-Klein modes. The central idea is to give the Kaluza-Klein tower a new role: not only as a spectrum of massive gravitational states, but as a set of communication carriers. Below the first KK threshold the channel is effectively four-dimensional and is mediated only by the massless graviton. Above threshold, massive KK modes open as additional propagating subchannels, and information may be encoded in their occupation pattern, relative phases, and arrival-time structure as well as in ordinary signal variables. The compactification determines the KK masses, wavefunctions, brane overlap factors, and propagation phases, which together define a multi-input multi-output (MIMO) channel matrix. In the resolved-mode limit, the tower yields approximate parallel subchannels, to which standard information-theoretic notions such as capacity bounds, water-filling, effective rank, and sparse occupancy codes apply. The production and detection of such signals are highly model-dependent and not assumed to be feasible with known technology. Nevertheless, the channel structure is well defined: a neighbouring brane-world could be separated from us by a microscopic distance in the compact space while remaining hidden because the only shared interaction is gravity. The first observable signature may not be a deliberate message, but the spectral and modal structure of the Kaluza-Klein tower itself, revealing partial information about the geometry of a nearby hidden world.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to exploit the analytic structure of superstring amplitudes to uncover deep connections between physical principles—such as unitarity, causality, and UV finiteness—and number-theoretic objects like the Riemann zeta function. Method: The authors compute the Mellin transform of the superstring four-point amplitude with respect to the Mandelstam variable $s$, keeping momentum transfer $t$ fixed. Conclusion: The Mellin transform provides a powerful bridge between string theory amplitudes and analytic number theory. This object exhibits remarkable mathematical properties as a result of physical attributes of the string amplitude. Conclusion: Resonance in GKZ systems provides a powerful algebraic framework for understanding and performing reductions of Feynman integrals. Abstract: Feynman integrals may be viewed as generalized hypergeometric functions, and specifically as solutions of GKZ systems of partial differential equations that typically exhibit resonance. Abstract: We continue the tree-level S-matrix bootstrap program for quantum gravity, now in ten-dimensional theories with half-maximal supersymmetry. Abstract: We show that axions coupled to photons in F-theory Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) satisfy the coupling-to-mass relation $g_{aγ}/m_a \leq C\, \frac{α_{\rm em}}{2π}\frac{1}{m_πf_π}$, with $C$ a calculable coefficient. These ALPs arise from the non-universal holomorphic threshold corrections to the gauge kinetic functions induced by the hypercharge flux. When gauge couplings approximately unify near the string scale, as required in phenomenologically viable models, the shift symmetries of these ALPs are broken by D-instantons whose action is controlled by the size of the threshold corrections to the gauge couplings. We consider possible loopholes to this result -- some of which could lead to $C\gg 1$ -- and argue that none of them allows for $g_{aγ}/m_a$ to be arbitrarily above the QCD axion prediction within regions of control for the effective action. The bound persists in models with large threshold corrections, where new incomplete GUT multiplets at intermediate energy scales are required. As a result, in the geometric regime, where the $α'$ expansion is under control, no ALP parametrically above the QCD axion band exists. Our results make F-theory GUTs falsifiable: finding an ALP far above the QCD band, for example discovering axion-induced cosmic birefringence, rules out F-theory GUTs in regimes of control of the effective theory.

Result: For any quiescent finite-radius branch with finite nonzero conformal factor, the mixed late-time equation enforces $J'(r_\infty)=0$, yielding $r_\infty = \sqrt{2}\,\ell$ universally. 对于任何具有有限非零共形因子的宁静有限半径分支，混合晚期方程强制 $J'(r_\infty)=0$，从而普适地给出 $r_\infty = \sqrt{2}\,\ell$。在态尾假设下，指数衰减的类光分支（可恢复强宇宙监督）被排除。一般的幂律类光分支 $e^{2ρ} \sim v^{-p}$（$p>1$）也被排除，仅剩边界情况 $p=2$。在FFN模型中，该 $p=2$ 分支要求 $s_φ = O(v^{-2})$ 且携带有限仿射通量。

Conclusion: The natural late-time outcome in the FFN framework is a remnant-like finite-radius configuration, while the only surviving null alternative is a highly constrained soft-null branch with $p=2$. 在FFN框架下，自然的晚期结局是残余类的有限半径构型，而唯一存留的类光替代方案是一个高度受限的 $p=2$ 软类光分支。这表明，当正确考虑来自四维约化的dilaton-物质耦合时，现实的二维黑洞蒸发终点倾向于非奇异残余，而非剧烈奇点或一般的类光无穷远。

Abstract: Spherical reduction of four-dimensional minimally coupled matter yields a two-dimensional theory with dilaton-coupled matter rather than minimally coupled conformal matter. Replacing the Polyakov quantum sector by the dilaton-coupled anomaly model of Fabbri, Farese, and Navarro-Salas (FFN), we derive the corresponding semiclassical field equations and classify the asymptotically allowed late branches at finite radius. For finite-radius null branches satisfying the stated state-tail assumptions, the ordinary strong-cosmic-censorship-restoring exponential null boundary is excluded. Generic power-law branches $e^{2ρ}\sim v^{-p}$ with $p>1$ are likewise excluded, except for the borderline case $p=2$, which is the only remaining null loophole of this type. In the FFN model, the settled realization of this loophole carries finite affine flux and requires the stronger state-tail decay $s_φ=O(v^{-2})$. The natural finite-radius outcome is remnant-like, while the surviving null branch is a highly constrained soft-null alternative.

  <summary>Details</summary>

去禁闭量子临界点（DQCP）作为超越Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson范式的相变典型例子，其本质长期存在争议，主要分为两种竞争性解释：伪临界性（pseudo-criticality，归因于复固定点附近的walking行为）与多临界性（multicriticality，即具有相关$SO(5)$单态标量的幺正固定点）。解决这一争议对理解二维量子磁体中的奇异量子临界行为至关重要。

Method: The authors apply the numerical conformal bootstrap in three dimensions, starting from the observation that large-scale Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) data nearly saturate existing bootstrap bounds. They impose a sparseness condition on the operator spectrum to sharpen the bootstrap constraints, resulting in a narrow cone-shaped allowed region in the 3D parameter space of scaling dimensions. Using the navigator algorithm, they locate the apex of this cone and extract extremal solutions corresponding to candidate CFTs. These solutions are then compared with both QMC and fuzzy sphere data.

作者在三维空间中应用数值共形自举方法，从大规模Quantum Monte Carlo（QMC）数据几乎饱和现有自举边界这一观察出发。他们对算符谱施加稀疏性条件（sparseness condition），以强化自举约束，从而在标度维度的三维参数空间中得到一个尖锐的锥形允许区域。利用navigator算法定位该锥的顶点，并提取对应的极值解（extremal solutions）作为候选CFT。随后将这些解与QMC和fuzzy sphere数据进行对比。

Result: The bootstrap cone apex yields a unitary CFT whose operator product expansion (OPE) coefficients and higher operator spectrum show striking agreement with both QMC simulations and fuzzy sphere constructions. 自举锥顶点对应一个幺正CFT，其算符积展开（OPE）系数和高阶算符谱与QMC模拟及fuzzy sphere构造结果高度一致。关键的是，该CFT包含一个相关的$SO(5)$单态标量算符，证实了多临界性情景的核心预言。共形自举、QMC和fuzzy sphere三种独立方法的汇聚，强有力地支持DQCP处存在真实的幺正固定点。

Conclusion: The work provides compelling evidence that the DQCP is a genuine unitary conformal fixed point with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet, thereby favoring the multicriticality scenario over pseudo-criticality. Abstract: The deconfined quantum critical point (DQCP) provides a prominent example of the unconventional phase transitions beyond the Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson paradigm and its nature has been controversial for decades. The DQCP has been extensively studied and the results lead to two opposite scenarios with pseudo-criticality or multicriticality. The pseudo-criticality is a prevailing scenario of DQCP which interprets the approximately scale invariant numerical results with the walking behavior near complex fixed points. In contrast, the multicriticality scenario conjectures the DQCP is a unitary fixed point with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet scalar. In this work we provide substantial evidence for the multicriticality scenario using conformal bootstrap. We start with the observation that the large scale Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) results nearly saturate the bootstrap bounds. After imposing suitable sparseness condition the bootstrap bound forms a sharp cone in the three-dimensional parameter space. The bootstrap cone is close to the QMC data. We use the navigator algorithm to locate the apex of the cone and extract the extremal solutions. We find striking consistencies between the bootstrap solutions and the fuzzy sphere data of DQCP, including the coefficients in the operator product expansions (OPEs) and the higher spectrum! The bootstrap cone unifies the QMC and the fuzzy sphere data into a unitary conformal field theory with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet scalar, thus strongly supporting the multicriticality scenario of DQCP. The agreement between the conformal bootstrap, QMC and fuzzy sphere results is a surprise towards solving DQCP and decoding the profound phase diagram of the two-dimensional quantum magnets.

### [61] [Ramond from Random: Weil-Petersson Volumes for Super-Riemann surfaces with NS Boundaries and R Punctures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09990)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper introduces a random matrix model framework to compute Weil-Petersson volumes $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$ of moduli spaces of ${N}=1$ supersymmetric Riemann surfaces with both Neveu-Schwarz (NS) boundaries and $2m$ Ramond (R) punctures. It provides closed-form expressions for these volumes, reveals new relations among them, and constructs the previously unknown spectral curve governing the system, enabling derivation via topological recursion.

本文引入一种随机矩阵模型框架，用于计算具有Neveu-Schwarz（NS）边界和$2m$个Ramond（R）穿孔的${N}=1$超对称黎曼曲面模空间的Weil-Petersson体积$V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$。该方法给出了这些体积的闭式表达式，揭示了它们之间的新关系，并构建了此前未知的谱曲线，从而可通过拓扑递归来推导这些体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard differential and algebraic geometry techniques struggle to compute Weil-Petersson volumes when Ramond punctures are present due to the degeneration of the superconformal structure at such points. The absence of a known spectral curve for the R-puncture sector has hindered progress in applying powerful tools like topological recursion. This motivates the development of an alternative approach that treats NS and R sectors symmetrically.
\n\n
当存在Ramond穿孔时，由于超共形结构在这些点退化，标准的微分几何与代数几何方法难以计算Weil-Petersson体积。R穿孔区域缺乏已知的谱曲线，阻碍了拓扑递归等强大工具的应用。这促使作者发展一种能对称处理NS与R区域的替代方法。

Method: The authors employ a recently developed random matrix model technique tailored for ${N}=1$ super-Riemann surfaces. This model naturally incorporates both NS boundaries and R punctures on equal footing. Using this framework, they derive closed-form formulae for the volumes $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$. Crucially, they construct the spectral curve associated with the inclusion of R punctures and apply Eynard-Orantin topological recursion to reproduce the volume formulas.
\n\n
作者采用一种为${N}=1$超黎曼曲面定制的近期发展的随机矩阵模型技术。该模型自然地将NS边界与R穿孔置于同等地位。利用此框架，他们推导出体积$V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$的闭式公式。关键在于，他们构建了包含R穿孔的谱曲线，并应用Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归来重现这些体积公式。

Result: The paper successfully computes explicit closed-form expressions for $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$, uncovers novel identities and relations among different volume sectors, and—most significantly—presents the first known spectral curve for the moduli space including Ramond punctures. This spectral curve enables the application of topological recursion to rederive the volumes systematically.
\n\n
本文成功计算出$V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$的显式闭式表达式，揭示了不同体积子空间之间的新恒等式与关系，并且最重要的是，首次提出了包含Ramond穿孔的模空间的谱曲线。该谱曲线使得可通过拓扑递归系统地重新推导这些体积。

Conclusion: The random matrix model approach provides a powerful and unified framework for studying the geometry of supersymmetric moduli spaces with mixed NS and R boundary conditions. By delivering the missing spectral curve and enabling topological recursion, it opens new avenues for exploring superstring perturbation theory, supergravity, and connections to integrable systems.
\n\n
随机矩阵模型方法为研究具有混合NS与R边界条件的超对称模空间几何提供了一个强大而统一的框架。通过提供缺失的谱曲线并启用拓扑递归，该方法为探索超弦微扰理论、超引力以及与可积系统的联系开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The Weil-Petersson (WP) volumes of the (compactified) moduli space of ${N}=1$ supersymmetric Riemann surfaces with Neveu-Schwarz (NS) boundaries are frequently discussed in the literature. Such surfaces can also have marked points called Ramond (R) punctures, where the superconformal structure degenerates. Computing the volumes when these R punctures are included is more challenging for the usual differential and algebraic geometry approaches, and they are therefore less well explored. In particular, the spectral curve describing the inclusion of R punctures is apparently unknown, so far. However, the right random matrix model approach can handle the NS and~R sectors on an equal footing. Such a construction is presented, showing how to use a recently developed technique to readily compute many closed-form formulae for $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$, the WP volumes for genus $g$ with $n$ NS-boundaries of geodesic lengths $b_i$ ($i{=}1,\ldots,n$), and $2m$ R-punctures. Several striking relations between volumes (and subsectors thereof) emerge naturally in this approach. Moreover, the hitherto missing spectral curve is presented, and its use for (re-)deriving the $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$ is demonstrated by using topological recursion.

</details>

It conjectures that all integral charges in $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ correspond to actual BPS states in quantum gravity, and that when $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ is moduli-independent, it equals the electric-magnetic dual of $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors employ a combination of supergravity analysis, charge lattice considerations, and duality arguments. 作者结合超引力分析、电荷格点考虑和对偶性论证。他们定义两个锥：$C_{\rm BPS-B}$（所有BPS膜电荷）和 $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$（支持红外BPS黑膜吸引子流的电荷）。利用电荷格点上的电-磁配对，他们在假设 $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ 与标量模无关的前提下，分析这两个锥之间的对偶关系。

Result: The paper establishes a conjectural correspondence: every integrally quantized charge in $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ is realized by a physical BPS state. Moreover, when $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ does not depend on moduli, it coincides with the dual cone of $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ under the electric-magnetic inner product, i.e., $C_{\rm BPS-B} = (C_{\rm BPS-BB})^\vee$. Conclusion: The distinction between $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ and $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ reveals subtle constraints in quantum gravity. Abstract: We study supersymmetric BPS branes (BPS-B) in supergravity theories. Some of these states are anticipated by BPS black-brane (BPS-BB) solutions of supergravity. In particular, we define and distinguish the cone generated by BPS branes from the subcone of charges that admit BPS black-brane attractor solutions in the infrared limit of the supergravity effective field theory. We denote these cones by $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ and $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$, respectively. We conjecture that, in any supersymmetric theory of quantum gravity, every integrally charged state lying in $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ is realized by a BPS state in the spectrum. Furthermore, we conjecture and present evidence that when $C_{\rm BPS-B}$ is moduli independent, it can be determined as the cone dual to the $C_{\rm BPS-BB}$ under the electric-magnetic pairing.

Method: The method combines brane engineering in Type IIA string theory with field-theoretic analysis. Matching of partition functions relies on assuming the 4d index equality. Additionally, they prove new confining dualities for two-node quivers using the tensor deconfinement technique—replacing a tensor field with a product of fundamentals and introducing a new gauge node.

Abstract: We derive new 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ dualities with $\mathrm{U}(N)$ gauge groups involving pairs of two-index tensors interacting through a quartic superpotential, (anti)-fundamentals and possibly an adjoint. A strong hint for their validity follows from T-duality on brane setups with O6 planes and stacks of multiple NS fivebranes. These setups engineer two families of 4d models known in the literature as mezzanine and balcony of type $A_k$ and $D_{k+2}$. Method: The authors construct a thermodynamic vector field on the space of black hole parameters whose zeros encode critical points: the Davies point (associated with specific heat divergence) and the Hawking–Page point (associated with confinement/deconfinement transition). They compute the winding numbers $w_{\rm D} = -1$ and $w_{\rm HP} = +1$ around these zeros on the elementary AdS branch. A signed first moment of this charge distribution is defined and normalized by Davies scales, yielding dimensionless universal ratios $C_S$ and $C_T$. The analysis is extended to Schwarzschild–AdS, Reissner–Nordström–AdS (grand-canonical), higher-dimensional charged static black holes, and Kerr–AdS at fixed angular velocity. A geometric barrier $B(d)$ is derived from the same reduced framework. Finally, they sketch a generalization involving defect operators in non-invertible or categorical symmetry sectors.

作者构建了一个在黑洞参数空间上的热力学矢量场，其零点编码了临界点：Davies点（与热容发散相关）和Hawking–Page点（与禁闭/去禁闭相变相关）。他们在基本AdS分支上计算出这些零点周围的卷绕数 $w_{\rm D} = -1$ 和 $w_{\rm HP} = +1$。定义了该电荷分布的带符号一阶矩，并用Davies标度进行归一化，从而得到无量纲的普适比值 $C_S$ 和 $C_T$。分析被推广到Schwarzschild–AdS、Reissner–Nordström–AdS（巨正则系综）、高维带电静态黑洞以及固定角速度的Kerr–AdS。从同一简化框架中导出了几何势垒 $B(d)$。最后，他们勾勒了涉及non-invertible或categorical对称性扇区中缺陷算符的推广方案。

These results hold consistently across Schwarzschild–AdS, grand-canonical Reissner–Nordström–AdS, higher-dimensional charged black holes, and rotating Kerr–AdS at fixed angular potential. 本文推导出控制电流传导与轴矢流传导的十三个输运系数函数在动量空间中的显式表达式。这些结果超越了传统流体力学的导数展开，包含了时间上的非局域效应。作为具体应用，作者给出了负磁阻效应和手征磁波的改进理论描述，其中包含了标准流体力学处理中缺失的有限频率与有限波数修正。

Abstract: Chiral plasma appears in several areas of physics, historically starting from primordial plasma in the early Universe, then in quark-gluon plasma produced in heavy ion collisions, and, more recently, in Dirac and Weyl semimetals. The major signature of the plasma is the non-conservation of the axial current due to the chiral anomaly and the emergence of new, anomaly-induced transport phenomena. Employing all-order gradient resummation, we write down constitutive relations for electric and axial currents parameterized by thirteen momentum- and magnetic- field-dependent transport coefficient functions. As an application, we revisit the phenomena of negative magnetoresistance and chiral magnetic waves, beyond the naive hydrodynamic limit.

Abstract: We study the pair creation phenomenon for a real scalar field $\varphi$ in the presence of a surface that undergoes time-dependent deformations, while imposing Dirichlet-like boundary conditions. Including terms up to fourth order in the departure of the surface from an infinite plane, we present results for the angular dependence of the emission rate for the vacuum-to-pair process as a function of the geometry and the dynamics of the surface, as well as of the momenta of the emitted pair. ### [68] [On Calabi-Yau Threefolds For Unified LVS Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10850)
*Pramod Shukla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper constructs a unified geometric framework within the LARGE Volume Scenario (LVS) of type IIB string compactifications, showing that three distinct Kähler moduli inflationary models—Fibre inflation, Poly-instanton inflation, and (Loop) Blow-up inflation—can all be realized on different orientifolds of a single Calabi-Yau (CY) threefold. The required CY must possess specific topological features: a K3- or ${\mathbb T}^4$-fibration, two diagonal del Pezzo divisors, and a 'Wilson' divisor (a ${\mathbb P}^1$-fibration over ${\mathbb T}^2$). A systematic scan of $\sim$100,000 CY threefolds with $1 \leq h^{1,1} \leq 6$ from the Kreuzer-Skarke database identifies viable candidates: 2 for $h^{1,1} \leq 4$, 14 for $h^{1,1}=5$, and 45 for $h^{1,1}=6$.

本文在IIB型弦论的LARGE Volume Scenario (LVS) 框架下构建了一个统一的几何框架，表明三种不同的Kähler模暴胀模型——Fibre inflation、Poly-instanton inflation和(Loop) Blow-up inflation——均可通过同一Calabi-Yau (CY) 三维流形的不同orientifold实现。所需的CY流形必须具备特定拓扑结构：K3或${\mathbb T}^4$纤维化、两个对角del Pezzo除子，以及一个“Wilson”除子（即${\mathbb P}^1$纤维化于${\mathbb T}^2$之上）。作者系统扫描了Kreuzer-Skarke数据库中$h^{1,1}$介于1到6之间的约10万个CY三维流形及其约百万个toric除子，发现满足条件的候选者：在$h^{1,1} \leq 4$时有2个，$h^{1,1}=5$时有14个，$h^{1,1}=6$时有45个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to unify seemingly distinct inflationary mechanisms in the LVS of type IIB string theory—namely Fibre, Poly-instanton, and (Loop) Blow-up inflation—under a single geometric origin. This addresses the question of whether these models are genuinely independent or merely different effective descriptions arising from the same underlying CY geometry with varying orientifold projections. Such unification simplifies the landscape of viable inflationary scenarios and clarifies their geometric prerequisites.
\n\n
动机在于统一IIB型弦论LARGE Volume Scenario (LVS) 中看似不同的暴胀机制——即Fibre inflation、Poly-instanton inflation和(Loop) Blow-up inflation——使其源于单一几何结构。这旨在回答这些模型究竟是本质独立的，还是仅由同一底层CY几何通过不同orientifold投影所导出的不同有效描述。这种统一简化了可行暴胀场景的景观，并澄清了其几何前提条件。

Method: The authors perform a systematic topological scan of Calabi-Yau threefolds constructed as hypersurfaces in toric fourfolds, derived from triangulations of 4D reflexive polytopes in the Kreuzer-Skarke database, restricting to those with Hodge number $1 \leq h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) \leq 6$. For each CY, they analyze the topology of all toric divisors to identify those satisfying three key geometric criteria: (i) existence of a K3- or ${\mathbb T}^4$-fibration structure; (ii) presence of two "diagonal" del Pezzo divisors (typically dP$_k$ surfaces); and (iii) a non-rigid "Wilson" divisor realized as a ${\mathbb P}^1$-fibration over ${\mathbb T}^2$. The scan covers approximately 100,000 CY geometries and one million divisor topologies.
\n\n
作者系统地扫描了作为四维环面簇中超曲面构造的Calabi-Yau三维流形，这些流形源自Kreuzer-Skarke数据库中四维自反多面体的三角剖分，并限制Hodge数为$1 \leq h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) \leq 6$。对于每个CY流形，他们分析所有toric除子的拓扑结构，以识别满足三个关键几何条件的流形：(i) 具备K3或${\mathbb T}^4$纤维化结构；(ii) 存在两个“对角”del Pezzo除子（通常为dP$_k$曲面）；(iii) 存在一个非刚性的“Wilson”除子，其实现为${\mathbb P}^1$纤维化于${\mathbb T}^2$之上。该扫描覆盖了约10万个CY几何结构和约一百万个除子拓扑。

Result: The scan yields a small but significant set of Calabi-Yau threefolds meeting all geometric requirements. Specifically, they find 2 candidate CYs for $1 \leq h^{1,1} \leq 4$, 14 for $h^{1,1} = 5$, and 45 for $h^{1,1} = 6$. These CYs provide explicit geometric realizations where different orientifold choices can lead to the effective field theories corresponding to Fibre, Poly-instanton, or (Loop) Blow-up inflation within the same underlying compactification space.
\n\n
扫描结果得到了一小批但具有重要意义的满足全部几何要求的Calabi-Yau三维流形。具体而言，作者发现：在$1 \leq h^{1,1} \leq 4$范围内有2个候选CY流形，$h^{1,1} = 5$时有14个，$h^{1,1} = 6$时有45个。这些CY流形提供了明确的几何实现，其中不同的orientifold选择可在同一紧致化空间中导出对应于Fibre inflation、Poly-instanton inflation或(Loop) Blow-up inflation的有效场论。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that multiple prominent LVS inflationary scenarios are not fundamentally distinct but can emerge from a common Calabi-Yau geometric foundation through different orientifold projections. This unification reduces model proliferation and highlights the importance of specific CY topological features—fibrations, del Pezzo divisors, and Wilson divisors—as universal building blocks for diverse inflationary mechanisms in string cosmology. The identified CY candidates offer concrete targets for detailed phenomenological and moduli stabilization studies.
\n\n
本文表明，多种主流的LVS暴胀场景并非本质不同，而是可通过不同orientifold投影从共同的Calabi-Yau几何基础中涌现。这种统一减少了模型泛滥，并凸显了特定CY拓扑特征——纤维化结构、del Pezzo除子和Wilson除子——作为弦宇宙学中多样化暴胀机制的通用构建模块的重要性。所识别的CY候选者为后续详细的唯象学和模稳定化研究提供了具体目标。

Abstract: Fibre inflation, Poly-instanton inflation and (Loop) Blow-up inflation are among the most popular Kähler moduli based inflationary models realized in the standard LARGE volume scenarios (LVS). In this article, we present a unified framework in which all these three LVS inflationary models can be realized by using (different orientifolds of) a single Calabi-Yau (CY) threefold. In fact, the desired CY threefold needs to have a K3- or ${\mathbb T}^4$-fibration structure along with two diagonal del Pezzo divisors, and a so-called `Wilson' divisor which corresponds to a surface realized as a ${\mathbb P}^1$ fibration over ${\mathbb T}^2$s. For classification purpose, we perform a detailed scan of the CY geometries with $1 \leq h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) \leq 6$ that arise from the triangulation of the four-dimensional reflexive polytopes of the Kreuzer-Skarke database. In this regard, after scanning around 100,000 CY geometries and the corresponding topologies of around a million of toric divisors, we find two CY threefolds satisfying these requirements for $1 \leq h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) \leq 4$, while there are 14 and 45 candidate CY geometries for $h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) = 5$ and $h^{1,1}({\rm CY}) = 6$, respectively. We discuss the extended applications of such CY threefolds for cosmological model building in string theoretic frameworks.

</details>


### [69] [Spacetime from Operator Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10924)
*Vyshnav Mohan,Larus Thorlacius*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that spacetime geometry and Einstein's equations can emerge from the algebra of quantized matter field operators in the limit $G_N \\to 0$, without relying on the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy area law. It further shows how non-perturbative effects at finite $N$ in holography can be captured by extending the type III von Neumann algebra with its modular Hamiltonian, and that a random matrix completion followed by ensemble averaging yields a type I algebra whose structure encodes black hole microstates and reproduces the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy with logarithmic corrections.
\n\n
本文表明，在 $G_N \\to 0$ 极限下，时空几何和爱因斯坦方程可以从量子化物质场算符代数中涌现出来，而无需依赖贝肯斯坦-霍金熵的面积律。文章进一步指出，全息理论在有限 $N$ 下的非微扰效应可通过将 type III von Neumann 代数与其 modular Hamiltonian 扩张来刻画；对该扩张代数进行随机矩阵理论补全并系综平均后，得到一个 type I von Neumann 代数，其结构编码了黑洞微观态，并复现了带有对数修正的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to establish a rigorous operator-algebraic foundation for emergent gravity, bypassing thermodynamic assumptions like the area law used in Jacobson's original derivation. The authors aim to provide criteria for when a quantum theory admits a semiclassical gravitational dual and to model finite-$N$ holographic effects through non-perturbative completions of semiclassical algebras.
\n\n
动机在于为涌现引力建立严格的算符代数基础，绕过 Jacobson 原始推导中使用的面积律等热力学假设。作者旨在提供判定一个量子理论是否具有半经典引力对偶的标准，并通过半经典代数的非微扰补全来建模有限 $N$ 全息效应。

Method: The method involves taking the $G_N \\to 0$ limit of quantum field theory on curved spacetime to recover geometric data from the operator algebra of matter fields. The type III von Neumann algebra associated with local regions is extended by adjoining its modular Hamiltonian. A random matrix theory (RMT) ansatz is then used to complete this enlarged algebra, followed by ensemble averaging to obtain a type I algebra. The minimal projectors of this type I algebra are interpreted as approximating black hole microstates.
\n\n
方法包括在弯曲时空上取量子场论的 $G_N \\to 0$ 极限，从物质场的算符代数中恢复几何数据。与局域区域相关的 type III von Neumann 代数通过添加其 modular Hamiltonian 进行扩张。随后采用随机矩阵理论（RMT）假设对该扩张代数进行补全，并通过系综平均得到一个 type I 代数。该 type I 代数的极小投影算符被解释为近似黑洞微观态。

Result: The full non-linear Einstein equations are derived purely from operator-algebraic assumptions in the $G_N \\to 0$ limit. For an eternal black hole, the dimension of the resulting type I von Neumann algebra matches the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S_{\\text{BH}}$ up to universal logarithmic corrections. Additionally, operator complexity in the boundary theory serves as a diagnostic for the breakdown of bulk effective field theory.
\n\n
在 $G_N \\to 0$ 极限下，仅基于算符代数假设就推导出了完整的非线性爱因斯坦方程。对于永恒黑洞，所得 type I von Neumann 代数的维数与贝肯斯坦-霍金熵 $S_{\\text{BH}}$ 一致，并包含普适的对数修正。此外，边界理论中算符的复杂度可作为体有效场论失效的诊断工具。

Conclusion: Gravity and spacetime geometry can be understood as emergent phenomena encoded in the structure of operator algebras of quantum matter. The transition from type III to type I von Neumann algebras via non-perturbative completion and ensemble averaging provides a concrete mechanism for recovering black hole microstates and their entropy from semiclassical data, offering a new perspective on the quantum origin of spacetime.
\n\n
引力和时空几何可被理解为编码在量子物质算符代数结构中的涌现现象。通过非微扰补全和系综平均实现从 type III 到 type I von Neumann 代数的转变，为从半经典数据中恢复黑洞微观态及其熵提供了具体机制，从而为时空的量子起源提供了新视角。

Abstract: Under suitable assumptions, geometric objects such as the spacetime metric and curvature tensor can be reconstructed from the algebra of operators of quantized matter fields in the limit of vanishing Newton's constant. In this framework, the full non-linear Einstein equations can be expressed in the language of operator algebras, extending Jacobson's derivation without invoking the area law for Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. These assumptions can then be used as a criterion for determining whether the semiclassical limit of a given quantum theory admits an emergent gravitational description. Going in the other direction, the discrete spectrum of a holographic theory at finite N can be modelled by adding non-perturbative corrections to semiclassical operator algebras. The type III von Neumann algebra that arises in the vanishing Newton's constant limit can be enlarged by adjoining its modular Hamiltonian. A random matrix theory completion of this enlarged algebra, followed by ensemble averaging, results in a type I von Neumann algebra whose minimal projectors approximate those of the underlying microstates. In the case of an eternal black hole, the dimension of the type I algebra equals the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy with universal logarithmic corrections. The complexity of probe operators in the boundary theory provides a diagnostic of the validity of the corresponding bulk semiclassical effective field theory.

</details>


### [70] [Supersymmetry of the static Reissner-Nordström black hole in Bertotti-Robinson ($\mathrm{AdS}_2 \times \mathbb{S}^2$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11101)
*Andrea Di Pinto,Adriano Viganò*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates supersymmetric properties of charged, accelerating black holes in a Bertotti-Robinson universe within $N=2$, $D=4$ supergravity. It derives the conditions for supersymmetry, computes explicit Killing spinors, confirms BPS saturation, determines the black hole mass via the BPS bound, analyzes thermodynamics, and generalizes the extremal solution to include a cosmological constant.
\n\n
本文研究了在 $N=2$、$D=4$ 超引力框架下，嵌入 Bertotti-Robinson 宇宙中的带电加速黑洞的超对称性质。文章推导了超对称条件，显式计算了 Killing spinor，验证了解满足 BPS bound，利用该结果计算了黑洞质量并分析其热力学性质，最后将极值解推广至包含宇宙学常数的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how supersymmetry manifests in non-trivial black hole spacetimes—specifically those that are both charged and accelerating—and how such configurations fit within the framework of $N=2$, $D=4$ supergravity. The Bertotti-Robinson geometry provides a natural near-horizon limit for extremal black holes, making it an ideal setting to explore BPS states and their thermodynamic implications. Including acceleration and a cosmological constant extends the scope to more realistic or generalized gravitational backgrounds.
\n\n
动机源于理解超对称性如何在非平凡黑洞时空中体现——特别是同时带电且加速的构型——以及这些构型如何契合 $N=2$、$D=4$ 超引力的理论框架。Bertotti-Robinson 几何为极值黑洞提供了自然的近视界极限，是探索 BPS 态及其热力学含义的理想背景。引入加速度和宇宙学常数可将研究推广至更现实或更一般的引力背景。

Method: The authors first review the known solution for a charged, accelerating black hole in a Bertotti-Robinson universe. They then impose the supersymmetry condition by solving the gravitino and gaugino variations in $N=2$, $D=4$ supergravity, which leads to first-order differential equations for the Killing spinors. By analyzing these equations, they derive the constraints required for preserved supersymmetry. Using the resulting BPS condition, they compute the black hole mass and study its thermodynamic quantities (e.g., entropy, temperature). Finally, they generalize the extremal solution by incorporating a non-zero cosmological constant into the field equations.
\n\n
作者首先回顾了 Bertotti-Robinson 宇宙中带电加速黑洞的已知解。随后在 $N=2$、$D=4$ 超引力中通过求解 gravitino 和 gaugino 的超对称变分方程，施加超对称条件，得到关于 Killing spinor 的一阶微分方程。通过对这些方程的分析，推导出保持超对称所需的约束条件。利用所得 BPS 条件计算黑洞质量并研究其热力学量（如熵、温度）。最后，通过在场方程中引入非零宇宙学常数，将极值解进行推广。

Result: The paper demonstrates that the charged, accelerating black hole solution preserves supersymmetry only when specific algebraic and differential constraints on the parameters (charge, acceleration, and background fields) are satisfied. Explicit Killing spinors are constructed, confirming that the solution is 1/2-BPS. The mass is shown to saturate the BPS bound: $M = |Q|$, where $Q$ is the electric charge. Thermodynamic analysis reveals zero Hawking temperature, consistent with extremality. The generalized solution with cosmological constant maintains BPS properties under modified constraints.
\n\n
论文表明，只有当参数（电荷、加速度和背景场）满足特定代数与微分约束时，带电加速黑洞解才保持超对称性。作者显式构造了 Killing spinor，证实该解为 1/2-BPS。质量满足 BPS bound：$M = |Q|$，其中 $Q$ 为电荷。热力学分析显示 Hawking 温度为零，符合极值性。包含宇宙学常数的推广解在修正后的约束下仍保持 BPS 性质。

Conclusion: The study confirms that supersymmetric, charged, accelerating black holes can exist in a Bertotti-Robinson universe within $N=2$, $D=4$ supergravity, provided stringent BPS conditions are met. The explicit construction of Killing spinors and verification of BPS saturation solidify the link between supersymmetry and extremal black hole thermodynamics. The inclusion of a cosmological constant extends the applicability of these results to asymptotically (A)dS spacetimes, opening avenues for further study in gauged supergravity.
\n\n
本研究证实，在 $N=2$、$D=4$ 超引力中，只要满足严格的 BPS 条件，Bertotti-Robinson 宇宙中可以存在超对称的带电加速黑洞。Killing spinor 的显式构造及 BPS 饱和的验证强化了超对称性与极值黑洞热力学之间的联系。引入宇宙学常数将结果推广至渐近 (A)dS 时空，为规范超引力中的进一步研究开辟了路径。

Abstract: We examine supersymmetry of charged and accelerating black holes embedded in a Bertotti-Robinson universe, in the context of $N=2$, $D=4$ supergravity. After a review of the solution, we study the constraints that guarantee supersymmetry and explicitly compute the Killing spinors of the spacetime. We show that the supersymmetric solution saturates the BPS bound, and we use this result to compute the mass of the black hole and to analyze the thermodynamics of the solution. Finally, we present a generalization of the extremal solution to include the cosmological constant.

</details>
